import CodeBlock from "./CodeBlock";
import GenerationIndicator from "./GenerationIndicator";
import { MarkdownText, parseContent } from "./MarkdownText";
import type { ResolvedMode } from "../modelRouting";
import type { AttachmentReference } from "./FileAttachments";

export type ChatMessage = {
  id?: number;
  dbId?: string;
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  content: string;
  promptContent?: string;
  attachments?: AttachmentReference[];
  modeUsed?: ResolvedMode;
  modelUsed?: string;
  timestamp: string;
  durationSec?: string;
  includeInHistory?: boolean;
  turnId?: number;
};

interface ChatMessageProps {
  message: ChatMessage;
  index: number;
  totalMessages: number;
  isGenerating: boolean;
  onPreview: (code: string, lang: string) => void;
  highlightCode: (code: string, lang: string) => string;
  onCopy: (content: string) => void;
  onEdit: (index: number) => void;
}

export default function ChatMessageItem({
  message,
  index,
  totalMessages,
  isGenerating,
  onPreview,
  highlightCode,
  onCopy,
  onEdit,
}: ChatMessageProps) {
  const isLastAssistant = isGenerating && index === totalMessages - 1;
  const assistantLabel = isLastAssistant && !message.content ? "Sonne думає" : "Sonne";

  const copyContent = message.promptContent ??
    message.content.replace(/\n\n📎 .*$/, "");

  return (
    <div className={`msg ${message.role}`}>
      <div className="msg-meta">
        <span className="msg-role-label">{message.role === "user" ? "Ти" : assistantLabel}</span>
        <span className="msg-time">
          {message.timestamp}
          {message.durationSec ? ` · ${message.durationSec} с` : ""}
        </span>
      </div>

      {message.role === "user" ? (
        <>
          <div>{message.content}</div>
          <div className="msg-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="msg-action-btn"
              onClick={() => onCopy(copyContent)}
              title="Копіювати текст запиту"
              aria-label="Копіювати текст запиту"
            >
              <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="17" height="17"
                fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"
                strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                <rect x="8" y="8" width="13" height="13" rx="2" />
                <path d="M16 8V5a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v9a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3" />
              </svg>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="msg-action-btn"
              onClick={() => onEdit(index)}
              title="Редагувати повідомлення"
              aria-label="Редагувати повідомлення"
            >
              <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="17" height="17"
                fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"
                strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                <path d="M12 20h9" />
                <path d="M16.5 3.5a2.12 2.12 0 0 1 3 3L8 18l-4 1 1-4Z" />
              </svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          {message.content &&
            parseContent(message.content).map((part, partIndex) =>
              part.type === "code" ? (
                <CodeBlock key={partIndex} lang={part.lang} code={part.code}
                  onPreview={onPreview} highlightCode={highlightCode} />
              ) : (
                <MarkdownText content={part.value} key={partIndex} />
              ),
            )}
          {isLastAssistant && <GenerationIndicator />}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: src/components/CodeBlock.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { copyText } from "../clipboard";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

interface CodeBlockProps {
  lang: string;
  code: string;
  onPreview: (code: string, lang: string) => void;
  highlightCode: (code: string, lang: string) => string;
}

export default function CodeBlock({
  lang,
  code,
  onPreview,
  highlightCode,
}: CodeBlockProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const highlighted = useMemo(
    () => highlightCode(code, lang),
    [code, lang, highlightCode],
  );

  const canPreview = ["html", "markup", "svg", "css"].includes(lang);

  async function handleCopy() {
    try {
      await copyText(code);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
    } catch {
      // Clipboard access may be unavailable.
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="code-block">
      <div className="code-header">
        <span className="code-lang">{lang}</span>

        <div className="code-btns">
          {canPreview && (
            <button
              className="mini-btn"
              onClick={() => onPreview(code, lang)}
            >
              Прев'ю
            </button>
          )}

          <button className="mini-btn" onClick={handleCopy}>
            {copied ? "Скопійовано ✓" : "Копіювати"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <pre>
        <code dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: highlighted }} />
      </pre>
    </div>
  );
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: src/components/FileAttachments.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import { getCurrentWebview } from "@tauri-apps/api/webview";
import ArchiveBrowser, { type ArchiveContextItem } from "./ArchiveBrowser";
import { processStoredAttachment } from "../fileReader";

export interface AttachmentReference {
  id: string;
  chatId: string;
  messageId: string | null;
  attachmentVersionId: string;
  attachmentId: string;
  versionNumber: number;
  fileName: string;
  extension: string;
  mimeType: string;
  blobSha256: string;
  originalSize: number;
  position: number;
  purpose: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

interface FileAttachmentsProps {
  chatId: string;
  attachments: AttachmentReference[];
  onAttachmentsChange: (attachments: AttachmentReference[]) => void;
  onArchiveContent: (item: ArchiveContextItem) => void;
  onPreviewAttachment: (attachment: AttachmentReference) => void;
}

function formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024) {
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  }
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} GB`;
}

export default function FileAttachments({
  chatId,
  attachments,
  onAttachmentsChange,
  onArchiveContent,
  onPreviewAttachment,
}: FileAttachmentsProps) {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false);
  const importInProgress = useRef(false);
  const [archiveReferenceId, setArchiveReferenceId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const processingReferences = useRef(new Set<string>());

  const [processingStatus, setProcessingStatus] = useState<Record<string, "processing" | "failed" | "completed">>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;

    const pending = attachments.filter((attachment) => {
      if (/\.(zip|tar|gz|tgz)$/i.test(attachment.fileName)) return false;
      if (processingReferences.current.has(attachment.id)) return false;

      processingReferences.current.add(attachment.id);
      return true;
    });

    for (const attachment of pending) {
      setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [attachment.id]: "processing",
      }));

      void processStoredAttachment({
        id: attachment.id,
        chatId: attachment.chatId || chatId,
        fileName: attachment.fileName,
        mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
        originalSize: attachment.originalSize,
      })
        .then(() => {
          if (disposed) return;
          setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
            ...current,
            [attachment.id]: "completed",
          }));
        })
        .catch((cause) => {
          processingReferences.current.delete(attachment.id);
          if (disposed) return;

          setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
            ...current,
            [attachment.id]: "failed",
          }));

          const message =
            cause instanceof Error
              ? cause.message
              : "Не вдалося обробити вкладення.";
          setError(`${attachment.fileName}: ${message}`);
        });
    }



    return () => {
      disposed = true;
    };
  }, [attachments, chatId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let unlistenDrop: (() => void) | undefined;
    let unlistenImported: (() => void) | undefined;
    let unlistenError: (() => void) | undefined;

    async function setupListeners() {
      const importedListener = await listen<AttachmentReference[]>(
        "attachments-imported",
        (event) => {
          if (disposed) return;
          onAttachmentsChange(event.payload);
          setError("");
          setIsImporting(false);
          importInProgress.current = false;
        },
      );

      const errorListener = await listen<string>(
        "attachments-import-error",
        (event) => {
          if (disposed) return;
          setError(event.payload);
          setIsImporting(false);
          importInProgress.current = false;
        },
      );

      const dropListener = await getCurrentWebview().onDragDropEvent(
        (event) => {
          if (disposed) return;

          switch (event.payload.type) {
            case "enter":
            case "over":
              setIsDragging(true);
              break;

            case "leave":
              setIsDragging(false);
              break;

            case "drop": {
              setIsDragging(false);
              const paths = event.payload.paths;

              if (paths.length > 0) {
                void importPaths(paths);
              }
              break;
            }
          }
        },
      );

      if (disposed) {
        importedListener();
        errorListener();
        dropListener();
        return;
      }

      unlistenImported = importedListener;
      unlistenError = errorListener;
      unlistenDrop = dropListener;
    }

    void setupListeners().catch((cause) => {
      if (!disposed) {
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Не вдалося підключити обробники вкладень.",
        );
      }
    });

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      unlistenImported?.();
      unlistenError?.();
      unlistenDrop?.();
    };
  }, [chatId, onAttachmentsChange]);
  async function retryAttachment(attachment: AttachmentReference) {
    if (processingReferences.current.has(attachment.id)) return;

    processingReferences.current.add(attachment.id);
    setError("");
    setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [attachment.id]: "processing",
    }));

    try {
      await processStoredAttachment({
        id: attachment.id,
        chatId: attachment.chatId || chatId,
        fileName: attachment.fileName,
        mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
        originalSize: attachment.originalSize,
      });

      setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [attachment.id]: "completed",
      }));
    } catch (cause) {
      processingReferences.current.delete(attachment.id);
      setProcessingStatus((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [attachment.id]: "failed",
      }));

      const message =
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Не вдалося повторно обробити вкладення.";
      setError(`${attachment.fileName}: ${message}`);
    }
  }

  async function importPaths(paths: string[]) {
    if (importInProgress.current || paths.length === 0) return;

    importInProgress.current = true;
    setIsImporting(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      await invoke("import_files", { chatId, paths });
    } catch (cause) {
      importInProgress.current = false;
      setIsImporting(false);
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Не вдалося імпортувати файли.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function openFileDialog() {
    if (importInProgress.current) return;

    setError("");

    try {
      await invoke("import_attachments", { chatId });
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Не вдалося відкрити діалог вибору файлів.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function removeAttachment(referenceId: string) {
    setError("");

    try {
      await invoke("remove_attachment_reference", {
        referenceId,
      });

      const updated = await invoke<AttachmentReference[]>(
        "list_composer_attachments",
        { chatId },
      );

      onAttachmentsChange(updated);
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Не вдалося видалити вкладення.",
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={`file-attachments ${isDragging ? "is-dragging" : ""}`}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="file-attach-btn"
        onClick={() => void openFileDialog()}
        disabled={isImporting}
        title="Додати файли"
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true">＋</span> Додати файли
      </button>

      {attachments.length > 0 && (
        <div className="file-attachments-list">
          {attachments.map((attachment) => (
            <div className="file-attachment" key={attachment.id}>
              <div className="file-attachment-preview">
                <span className="file-attachment-name">
                  {attachment.fileName}
                </span>
                <span className="file-attachment-size">
                  {formatFileSize(attachment.originalSize)}
                </span>
                {processingStatus[attachment.id] === "processing" && (
                  <span className="file-attachments-status">Обробка...</span>
                )}
                {processingStatus[attachment.id] === "failed" && (
                  <>
                    <span className="file-attachments-error">Помилка обробки</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="file-attachment-open"
                      onClick={() => void retryAttachment(attachment)}
                    >
                      Повторити
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}

                {!/\.(zip|tar|gz|tgz)$/i.test(attachment.fileName) && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="file-attachment-open"
                    onClick={() => onPreviewAttachment(attachment)}
                    title="Попередній перегляд файлу"
                    aria-label={`Переглянути ${attachment.fileName}`}
                  >
                    Переглянути
                  </button>
                )}
                {/\.(zip|tar|gz|tgz)$/i.test(attachment.fileName) && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="file-attachment-open"
                    onClick={() => setArchiveReferenceId(attachment.id)}
                    title="Переглянути вміст архіву"
                    aria-label={`Переглянути архів ${attachment.fileName}`}
                  >
                    Переглянути
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="file-attachment-remove"
                onClick={() => void removeAttachment(attachment.id)}
                aria-label={`Видалити ${attachment.fileName}`}
                title="Видалити файл"
              >
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {archiveReferenceId && (
        <ArchiveBrowser
          key={`${chatId}:${archiveReferenceId}`}
          chatId={chatId}
          referenceId={archiveReferenceId}
          onClose={() => setArchiveReferenceId(null)}
    onAddToContext={onArchiveContent}
        />
      )}

      {isImporting && (
        <div className="file-attachments-status">Імпорт файлів...</div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="file-attachments-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {isDragging && (
        <div className="file-drop-overlay">
          Відпусти файли, щоб додати їх
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/FilePreviewPane.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import {
  loadStoredAttachment,
  readStoredAttachment,
} from "../fileReader";
import type { AttachmentReference } from "./FileAttachments";

interface DocumentSearchResult {
  chunkId: string;
  fileName: string;
  detectedFormat: string | null;
  content: string;
}

interface FilePreviewPaneProps {
  attachment: AttachmentReference;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function formatSize(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

export default function FilePreviewPane({
  attachment,
  onClose,
}: FilePreviewPaneProps) {
  const [content, setContent] = useState("");
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<DocumentSearchResult[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [searchError, setSearchError] = useState("");

  async function searchDocuments() {
    const query = searchQuery.trim();
    if (!query || searching) return;

    setSearching(true);
    setSearchError("");
    setSearchResults([]);

    try {
      const results = await invoke<DocumentSearchResult[]>(
        "search_attachment_documents",
        {
          chatId: attachment.chatId,
          query,
          limit: 30,
        },
      );
      setSearchResults(results);
      if (results.length === 0) setSearchError("Нічого не знайдено.");
    } catch (cause) {
      setSearchError(
        cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Не вдалося виконати пошук.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSearching(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let objectUrl: string | null = null;

    setContent("");
    setImageUrl(null);
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);

    const input = {
      id: attachment.id,
      chatId: attachment.chatId,
      fileName: attachment.fileName,
      mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
      originalSize: attachment.originalSize,
    };

    async function loadPreview() {
      try {
        const isImage =
          attachment.mimeType.toLowerCase().startsWith("image/") ||
          /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|bmp|tiff?|avif|heic|heif)$/i.test(
            attachment.fileName,
          );

        if (isImage) {
          const file = await loadStoredAttachment(input);
          objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
          if (!cancelled) setImageUrl(objectUrl);
        } else {
          const result = await readStoredAttachment(input);
          if (!cancelled) {
            setContent(
              result.content.trim()
                ? result.content
                : "Для цього файлу не вдалося отримати текстовий вміст.",
            );
          }
        }
      } catch (cause) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            cause instanceof Error
              ? cause.message
              : "Не вдалося відкрити попередній перегляд файлу.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadPreview();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (objectUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
    };
  }, [
    attachment.id,
    attachment.chatId,
    attachment.fileName,
    attachment.mimeType,
    attachment.originalSize,
  ]);

  return (
    <aside className="file-preview-pane" aria-label="Попередній перегляд файлу">
      <div className="file-preview-header">
        <span className="file-preview-title" title={attachment.fileName}>
          {attachment.fileName}
        </span>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="file-preview-close"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Закрити попередній перегляд"
          title="Закрити"
        >
          ×
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="file-preview-meta">
        {attachment.mimeType || "Невідомий формат"} · {formatSize(attachment.originalSize)}
      </div>
      <form
        className="file-preview-search"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          void searchDocuments();
        }}
      >
        <input
          type="search"
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(event) => setSearchQuery(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Пошук у документах чату…"
          aria-label="Пошук у документах чату"
          maxLength={512}
        />
        <button type="submit" disabled={searching || !searchQuery.trim()}>
          {searching ? "…" : "Знайти"}
        </button>
      </form>
      {(searchResults.length > 0 || searchError) && (
        <div className="file-preview-search-results" aria-live="polite">
          {searchError && <p className="file-preview-message">{searchError}</p>}
          {searchResults.map((result) => (
            <article className="file-preview-search-result" key={result.chunkId}>
              <strong title={result.fileName}>{result.fileName}</strong>
              <p>{result.content}</p>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="file-preview-content">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="file-preview-message">Завантаження попереднього перегляду…</p>
        ) : error ? (
          <p className="file-preview-message" role="alert">{error}</p>
        ) : imageUrl ? (
          <img src={imageUrl} alt={attachment.fileName} />
        ) : (
          <pre>{content}</pre>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: src/contextManager.ts
====================================================================================================
// ============================================================
// SONNE CONTEXT MANAGER (pure, UI-independent, no imports)
//
// Phase 2A: Context Budget + Smart History.
//
// Decides which part of the conversation is sent to the model. It works
// on a COPY of the history that is about to be sent; the conversation
// shown in the UI and stored by Sonne is never touched.
//
// All token numbers here are LOCAL ESTIMATES, not Ollama token counts.
// The real number is `prompt_eval_count` on Ollama's final chunk.
// ============================================================

export type ContextMessage = { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string };

/** Structural subset of BackendConfig (modelRouting.ts), so this file needs no imports. */
export type ContextModelConfig = {
  ollamaModel: string;
  options?: { num_ctx: number; num_predict: number };
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CONFIGURATION (single place to maintain)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Context windows (num_ctx) for models whose window is NOT sent per request,
 * i.e. models with a Sonne Modelfile. Keyed by exact Ollama model name.
 * Fill in from each Modelfile's `PARAMETER num_ctx`; do not guess.
 * Models with request-level `options.num_ctx` (Pro Instruct) use that value
 * automatically, so it can never drift from what Ollama is actually given.
 */
export const MODEL_CONTEXT_WINDOWS: Readonly<Record<string, number>> = {
  "sonne-gemma:latest": 8192,
  "sonne-pro-ai:latest": 12288,
};

/** Used for any model without a known window. Equals Ollama's classic default. */
export const FALLBACK_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 4096;

/** Share of the window kept free for the answer (more when the model thinks). */
export const OUTPUT_RESERVE_RATIO = 0.25;
export const OUTPUT_RESERVE_RATIO_THINKING = 0.35;
export const MIN_OUTPUT_RESERVE_TOKENS = 512;

/** Extra headroom because the estimator is approximate. */
export const SAFETY_MARGIN_RATIO = 0.05;

/** Rough per-message cost of role/template tokens. */
export const MESSAGE_OVERHEAD_TOKENS = 4;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TOKEN ESTIMATION (estimate only; deliberately on the high side)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Weighted character count, no tokenizer. Roughly: Cyrillic 2 chars/token,
 * Latin ~3 chars/token, punctuation/digits/symbols 2 chars/token, whitespace
 * cheap, other scripts 1 token/char. Not exact; tuned to over- not under-count.
 */
export function estimateTokens(text: string): number {
  let weight = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text.charCodeAt(i);

    if (c === 32 || c === 10 || c === 9 || c === 13) weight += 0.15;
    else if ((c >= 65 && c <= 90) || (c >= 97 && c <= 122)) weight += 0.32;
    else if (c >= 0x0400 && c <= 0x052f) weight += 0.5; // Cyrillic
    else if (c < 128) weight += 0.5; // digits, punctuation, symbols
    else if (c >= 0xd800 && c <= 0xdfff) weight += 0.75; // surrogate half (emoji etc.)
    else weight += 1; // CJK and other scripts

  }

  return Math.ceil(weight);
}

export function estimateMessageTokens(message: { content: string }): number {
  return estimateTokens(message.content) + MESSAGE_OVERHEAD_TOKENS;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CONTEXT BUDGET
// ------------------------------------------------------------

export type ContextBudget = {
  contextWindow: number;
  windowSource: "request-options" | "model-table" | "fallback";
  outputReserve: number;
  safetyMargin: number;
  /** Max estimated tokens for system prompt + history + current message. */
  inputBudget: number;
};

export function resolveContextBudget(
  config: ContextModelConfig,
  thinking: boolean,
): ContextBudget {
  let contextWindow = FALLBACK_CONTEXT_WINDOW;
  let windowSource: ContextBudget["windowSource"] = "fallback";

  if (config.options && config.options.num_ctx > 0) {
    contextWindow = config.options.num_ctx;
    windowSource = "request-options";
  } else if (MODEL_CONTEXT_WINDOWS[config.ollamaModel] > 0) {
    contextWindow = MODEL_CONTEXT_WINDOWS[config.ollamaModel];
    windowSource = "model-table";
  }

  const ratio = thinking ? OUTPUT_RESERVE_RATIO_THINKING : OUTPUT_RESERVE_RATIO;
  let outputReserve = Math.max(MIN_OUTPUT_RESERVE_TOKENS, Math.floor(contextWindow * ratio));

  // A smaller configured output limit needs no larger reserve.
  if (config.options && config.options.num_predict > 0) {
    outputReserve = Math.min(outputReserve, config.options.num_predict);
  }

  const safetyMargin = Math.ceil(contextWindow * SAFETY_MARGIN_RATIO);
  const inputBudget = Math.max(0, contextWindow - outputReserve - safetyMargin);

  return { contextWindow, windowSource, outputReserve, safetyMargin, inputBudget };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SMART HISTORY (recent-first, whole turns, deterministic)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

export type HistorySelectionStatus =
  | "within-budget" // everything fits; history unchanged
  | "trimmed" // oldest turns omitted
  | "fixed-content-exceeds-budget"; // system prompt + current message alone exceed the budget

export type ContextStats = {
  status: HistorySelectionStatus;
  /** True when any history was omitted from the model input. */
  optimized: boolean;
  contextWindow: number;
  windowSource: ContextBudget["windowSource"];
  inputBudget: number;
  outputReserve: number;
  // All token values below are ESTIMATES (not Ollama's prompt_eval_count).
  estFixedTokens: number; // system prompt + current user message
  estOriginalHistoryTokens: number;
  estSelectedHistoryTokens: number;
  estRemovedHistoryTokens: number;
  estInputTokens: number; // fixed + selected history
  totalHistoryMessages: number;
  selectedHistoryMessages: number;
  omittedHistoryMessages: number;
};

export type HistorySelection = {
  /** New array; the input array and its message objects are never modified. */
  history: ContextMessage[];
  stats: ContextStats;
};

export function selectHistory(
  history: readonly ContextMessage[],
  input: { systemPrompt: string; userPrompt: string; budget: ContextBudget },
): HistorySelection {
  const { budget } = input;
  const costs = history.map(estimateMessageTokens);
  const originalTokens = costs.reduce((sum, cost) => sum + cost, 0);
  const fixedTokens =
    estimateMessageTokens({ content: input.systemPrompt }) +
    estimateMessageTokens({ content: input.userPrompt });
  const available = budget.inputBudget - fixedTokens;

  let keepFrom = 0;
  let status: HistorySelectionStatus = "within-budget";

  if (available < 0) {
    // System prompt and current message are never cut. Send them as they are
    // without history and let the caller surface the overflow.
    keepFrom = history.length;
    status = "fixed-content-exceeds-budget";
  } else if (originalTokens > available) {
    status = "trimmed";
    keepFrom = history.length;

    // Walk back one turn at a time (a user message plus the replies that
    // follow it). A turn that does not fit stops the walk, so what is kept
    // is always a contiguous, whole-turn tail of the conversation.
    let used = 0;
    let end = history.length;

    while (end > 0) {
      let start = end - 1;

      while (start > 0 && history[start].role !== "user") start--;

      // Replies with no user message before them would be orphaned.
      if (history[start].role !== "user") break;

      let turnCost = 0;

      for (let i = start; i < end; i++) turnCost += costs[i];
      if (used + turnCost > available) break;

      used += turnCost;
      keepFrom = start;
      end = start;
    }
  }

  const selected = history.slice(keepFrom).map((message) => ({
    role: message.role,
    content: message.content,
  }));
  const selectedTokens = costs.slice(keepFrom).reduce((sum, cost) => sum + cost, 0);
  const omitted = history.length - selected.length;

  return {
    history: selected,
    stats: {
      status,
      optimized: omitted > 0,
      contextWindow: budget.contextWindow,
      windowSource: budget.windowSource,
      inputBudget: budget.inputBudget,
      outputReserve: budget.outputReserve,
      estFixedTokens: fixedTokens,
      estOriginalHistoryTokens: originalTokens,
      estSelectedHistoryTokens: selectedTokens,
      estRemovedHistoryTokens: originalTokens - selectedTokens,
      estInputTokens: fixedTokens + selectedTokens,
      totalHistoryMessages: history.length,
      selectedHistoryMessages: selected.length,
      omittedHistoryMessages: omitted,
    },
  };
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: src/documentSummaries.ts
====================================================================================================
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import type { ChatRequest } from "ollama/browser";
import { createChatStream, OLLAMA_HOST } from "./ollamaClient";
import type { RuntimeOptions } from "./modelRouting";

const SUMMARY_VERSION = "sonne-map-reduce-v1";
const MAP_GROUP_SIZE = 8;
const REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE = 6;
const DB_BATCH_SIZE = 128;
const SAVE_BATCH_SIZE = 16;
const MAX_REDUCE_STAGES = 32;

type SummaryChunk = {
  chunkId: string;
  ordinal: number;
  content: string;
  chunkType: string;
  chunkMetadata: string | null;
  sectionType: string | null;
  sectionTitle: string | null;
  sectionMetadata: string | null;
};

type SummaryPart = {
  ordinal: number;
  content: string;
  sourceChunkCount: number;
  modelName: string;
  summarizerVersion: string;
};

type SummaryRecord = {
  documentId: string;
  summary: string;
  modelName: string;
  summarizerVersion: string;
  status: string;
  sourceChunkCount: number;
  updatedAt: number;
};

type SummaryPartInput = {
  ordinal: number;
  content: string;
  sourceChunkCount: number;
};

async function readChunks(
  documentId: string,
  onBatch: (chunks: SummaryChunk[]) => Promise<void>,
): Promise<number> {
  let cursor = -1;
  let total = 0;

  while (true) {
    const batch = await invoke<SummaryChunk[]>(
      "get_document_summary_chunk_batch",
      {
        documentId,
        afterOrdinal: cursor,
        limit: DB_BATCH_SIZE,
      },
    );

    if (batch.length === 0) break;

    const nextCursor = batch[batch.length - 1].ordinal;
    if (nextCursor <= cursor) {
      throw new Error("Некоректний курсор читання фрагментів документа.");
    }

    await onBatch(batch);
    total += batch.length;
    cursor = nextCursor;

    if (batch.length < DB_BATCH_SIZE) break;
  }

  return total;
}

async function readParts(
  documentId: string,
  stage: number,
): Promise<SummaryPart[]> {
  let cursor = -1;
  const result: SummaryPart[] = [];

  while (true) {
    const batch = await invoke<SummaryPart[]>(
      "get_document_summary_parts",
      {
        documentId,
        stage,
        afterOrdinal: cursor,
        limit: DB_BATCH_SIZE,
      },
    );

    if (batch.length === 0) break;

    const nextCursor = batch[batch.length - 1].ordinal;
    if (nextCursor <= cursor) {
      throw new Error("Некоректний курсор читання проміжного резюме.");
    }

    result.push(...batch);
    cursor = nextCursor;

    if (batch.length < DB_BATCH_SIZE) break;
  }

  return result;
}

async function clearStagesFrom(
  documentId: string,
  firstStage: number,
): Promise<void> {
  for (let stage = firstStage; stage <= MAX_REDUCE_STAGES; stage += 1) {
    await invoke("clear_document_summary_stage", {
      documentId,
      stage,
    });
  }
}

async function saveParts(
  documentId: string,
  stage: number,
  modelName: string,
  parts: SummaryPartInput[],
): Promise<void> {
  if (parts.length === 0) return;

  await invoke("save_document_summary_parts", {
    documentId,
    stage,
    modelName,
    summarizerVersion: SUMMARY_VERSION,
    parts,
  });
}

async function generateText(
  modelName: string,
  options: RuntimeOptions | undefined,
  systemPrompt: string,
  userPrompt: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
): Promise<string> {
  if (signal.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("Summary generation cancelled", "AbortError");
  }

  const request: ChatRequest & { stream: true } = {
    model: modelName,
    stream: true,
    think: false,
    messages: [
      { role: "system", content: systemPrompt },
      { role: "user", content: userPrompt },
    ],
    ...(options ? { options } : {}),
  };

  const active = await createChatStream(OLLAMA_HOST, request, signal);
  let result = "";

  try {
    for await (const chunk of active.stream) {
      if (signal.aborted) {
        throw new DOMException("Summary generation cancelled", "AbortError");
      }

      result += chunk.message?.content || "";
    }
  } finally {
    active.dispose();
  }

  const normalized = result.trim();
  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error("Модель повернула порожнє резюме документа.");
  }

  return normalized;
}

function chunkSource(chunk: SummaryChunk): string {
  return [
    `Тип фрагмента: ${chunk.chunkType}`,
    chunk.sectionType ? `Тип розділу: ${chunk.sectionType}` : "",
    chunk.sectionTitle ? `Назва розділу: ${chunk.sectionTitle}` : "",
    chunk.chunkMetadata ? `Метадані фрагмента: ${chunk.chunkMetadata}` : "",
    chunk.sectionMetadata ? `Метадані розділу: ${chunk.sectionMetadata}` : "",
    "Вміст:",
    chunk.content,
  ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
}

const MAP_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Ти — модуль точного аналізу документів Sonne.
Створи змістовне проміжне резюме наданих фрагментів.
Зберігай конкретні факти, імена, дати, числа, суми, одиниці виміру,
умови, причинно-наслідкові зв'язки, висновки, обмеження та важливі деталі.
Зберігай структуру й назви розділів, якщо вони є.
Не вигадуй відсутньої інформації. Не підміняй невідоме припущеннями.
Не згадуй, що це проміжне резюме. Відповідай мовою документа.`;

const REDUCE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Ти — модуль узагальнення документів Sonne.
Об'єднай надані проміжні резюме в одне цілісне резюме.
Не втрачай факти, числа, дати, імена, умови, висновки та важливі винятки.
Усувай дублювання, але не відкидай унікальну інформацію.
Зберігай логічну структуру та зв'язки між розділами.
Не вигадуй відсутньої інформації. Відповідай мовою документа.`;

function validParts(
  parts: SummaryPart[],
  expectedCount: number,
  expectedSourceCounts: number[],
  modelName: string,
): boolean {
  if (parts.length > expectedCount) return false;

  for (const part of parts) {
    if (
      part.ordinal < 0 ||
      part.ordinal >= expectedCount ||
      part.modelName !== modelName ||
      part.summarizerVersion !== SUMMARY_VERSION ||
      part.sourceChunkCount !== expectedSourceCounts[part.ordinal] ||
      !part.content.trim()
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

export async function generateDocumentSummary(
  documentId: string,
  modelName: string,
  options?: RuntimeOptions,
  externalSignal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<string> {
  if (!documentId || documentId.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("Некоректний ID документа.");
  }

  if (!modelName || modelName.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("Не визначено модель для резюме документа.");
  }

  const controller = externalSignal
    ? null
    : new AbortController();
  const signal = externalSignal ?? controller!.signal;

  const existingSummary = await invoke<SummaryRecord | null>(
    "get_document_summary",
    { documentId },
  );

  if (
    existingSummary?.status === "completed" &&
    existingSummary.modelName === modelName &&
    existingSummary.summarizerVersion === SUMMARY_VERSION &&
    existingSummary.summary.trim()
  ) {
    return existingSummary.summary;
  }

  // First pass: count all source chunks without loading the entire document.
  const totalChunks = await readChunks(documentId, async () => {});
  if (totalChunks === 0) {
    throw new Error("Документ не містить текстових фрагментів для резюме.");
  }

  const mapCount = Math.ceil(totalChunks / MAP_GROUP_SIZE);
  const mapSourceCounts = Array.from(
    { length: mapCount },
    (_, index) => Math.min(
      MAP_GROUP_SIZE,
      totalChunks - index * MAP_GROUP_SIZE,
    ),
  );

  let mapParts = await readParts(documentId, 0);

  if (!validParts(mapParts, mapCount, mapSourceCounts, modelName)) {
    await clearStagesFrom(documentId, 0);
    mapParts = [];
  }

  const existingMap = new Map(mapParts.map((part) => [part.ordinal, part]));
  let pendingMap: SummaryPartInput[] = [];
  let mapOrdinal = 0;
  let chunkGroup: SummaryChunk[] = [];

  const flushMapGroup = async () => {
    if (chunkGroup.length === 0) return;

    const ordinal = mapOrdinal++;
    if (!existingMap.has(ordinal)) {
      const content = await generateText(
        modelName,
        options,
        MAP_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        `Проаналізуй групу ${ordinal + 1} з ${mapCount} документа.\n\n${
          chunkGroup.map(chunkSource).join("\n\n---\n\n")
        }`,
        signal,
      );

      pendingMap.push({
        ordinal,
        content,
        sourceChunkCount: chunkGroup.length,
      });

      if (pendingMap.length >= SAVE_BATCH_SIZE) {
        await saveParts(documentId, 0, modelName, pendingMap);
        pendingMap = [];
      }
    }

    chunkGroup = [];
  };

  // Second pass: Map. Each result is persisted before moving on.
  await readChunks(documentId, async (batch) => {
    for (const chunk of batch) {
      chunkGroup.push(chunk);
      if (chunkGroup.length === MAP_GROUP_SIZE) {
        await flushMapGroup();
      }
    }
  });

  await flushMapGroup();
  await saveParts(documentId, 0, modelName, pendingMap);

  let currentParts = await readParts(documentId, 0);

  if (
    currentParts.length !== mapCount ||
    !validParts(currentParts, mapCount, mapSourceCounts, modelName)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Не вдалося повністю сформувати Map-рівень документа.");
  }

  // Reduce: merge groups hierarchically until exactly one part remains.
  let stage = 1;

  while (currentParts.length > 1) {
    if (stage > MAX_REDUCE_STAGES) {
      throw new Error("Перевищено максимальну глибину Reduce.");
    }

    const expectedCount = Math.ceil(
      currentParts.length / REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE,
    );

    const expectedSourceCounts = Array.from(
      { length: expectedCount },
      (_, index) =>
        currentParts
          .slice(
            index * REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE,
            (index + 1) * REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE,
          )
          .reduce((sum, part) => sum + part.sourceChunkCount, 0),
    );

    let stageParts = await readParts(documentId, stage);

    if (
      !validParts(
        stageParts,
        expectedCount,
        expectedSourceCounts,
        modelName,
      )
    ) {
      await clearStagesFrom(documentId, stage);
      stageParts = [];
    }

    const existingStage = new Map(
      stageParts.map((part) => [part.ordinal, part]),
    );

    let pending: SummaryPartInput[] = [];

    for (let index = 0; index < expectedCount; index += 1) {
      if (existingStage.has(index)) continue;

      const group = currentParts.slice(
        index * REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE,
        (index + 1) * REDUCE_GROUP_SIZE,
      );

      const content = await generateText(
        modelName,
        options,
        REDUCE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        `Об'єднай групу ${index + 1} з ${expectedCount}.\n\n${
          group.map((part, partIndex) =>
            `Проміжне резюме ${partIndex + 1}:\n${part.content}`,
          ).join("\n\n---\n\n")
        }`,
        signal,
      );

      pending.push({
        ordinal: index,
        content,
        sourceChunkCount: expectedSourceCounts[index],
      });

      if (pending.length >= SAVE_BATCH_SIZE) {
        await saveParts(documentId, stage, modelName, pending);
        pending = [];
      }
    }

    await saveParts(documentId, stage, modelName, pending);

    currentParts = await readParts(documentId, stage);

    if (
      currentParts.length !== expectedCount ||
      !validParts(
        currentParts,
        expectedCount,
        expectedSourceCounts,
        modelName,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(`Не вдалося повністю сформувати Reduce-рівень ${stage}.`);
    }

    stage += 1;
  }

  const finalPart = currentParts[0];

  if (!finalPart || finalPart.sourceChunkCount !== totalChunks) {
    throw new Error("Фінальне резюме не охоплює весь документ.");
  }

  await invoke("finalize_document_summary", {
    documentId,
    summary: finalPart.content,
    modelName,
    summarizerVersion: SUMMARY_VERSION,
    sourceChunkCount: totalChunks,
  });

  return finalPart.content;
}

FILE: src/fileReader.ts

import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { OfficeParser } from "officeparser";
import { createWorker } from "tesseract.js";

import { createEmbeddings, createVisionDescription, OLLAMA_HOST } from "./ollamaClient";
export type SupportedFileType =
  | "text"
  | "pdf"
  | "document"
  | "spreadsheet"
  | "presentation"
  | "epub"
  | "image";

export type ReadFileResult = {
  name: string;
  type: SupportedFileType;
  size: number;
  content: string;
};

const RESOURCE_ROOT = "/resources";

const PDF_WORKER = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/pdfjs/pdf.worker.min.mjs`;
const PDF_CMAPS = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/pdfjs/cmaps/`;
const PDF_DECODERS = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/pdfjs/wasm/`;

const OCR_WORKER = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/tesseract/worker.min.js`;
const OCR_CORE = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/tesseract/core/`;
const OCR_LANG = `${RESOURCE_ROOT}/tesseract/lang/`;

const TEXT_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  "txt", "md", "markdown", "json", "jsonc", "html", "htm",
  "css", "scss", "sass", "less", "js", "jsx", "mjs", "cjs",
  "ts", "tsx", "py", "java", "c", "cpp", "h", "hpp", "cs",
  "go", "rs", "php", "rb", "swift", "kt", "sql", "xml",
  "yaml", "yml", "toml", "ini", "sh", "bash", "zsh", "env",
  "log", "gitignore", "dockerfile", "vue", "svelte",
  "graphql", "gql", "svg",
]);

const IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp", "gif", "bmp",
  "tif", "tiff", "avif", "heic", "heif",
]);

const FILE_TYPES: Readonly<Record<string, SupportedFileType>> = {
  pdf: "pdf",

  docx: "document",
  odt: "document",
  rtf: "document",

  xlsx: "spreadsheet",
  ods: "spreadsheet",
  csv: "spreadsheet",
  tsv: "spreadsheet",

  pptx: "presentation",
  odp: "presentation",

  epub: "epub",
};

function extensionOf(name: string): string {
  const index = name.lastIndexOf(".");
  return index >= 0
    ? name.slice(index + 1).toLowerCase()
    : name.toLowerCase();
}

export function getFileType(file: File): SupportedFileType {
  const extension = extensionOf(file.name);

  if (FILE_TYPES[extension]) return FILE_TYPES[extension];
  if (IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.has(extension)) return "image";
  if (TEXT_EXTENSIONS.has(extension)) return "text";

  throw new Error(
    `Непідтримуваний формат файлу: ${extension || "unknown"}`,
  );
}

async function readTextFile(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return file.text();
}

async function readOfficeFile(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const extension = extensionOf(file.name);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());

  const ast = await OfficeParser.parseOffice(bytes, {
    fileType: (extension === "tsv" ? "csv" : extension) as "pdf" | "docx" | "odt" | "rtf" | "xlsx" | "ods" | "csv" | "pptx" | "odp" | "epub",
    allowRemoteAssets: false,

    pdfWorkerSrc: PDF_WORKER,
    pdfCMapUrl: PDF_CMAPS,
    pdfDecoderUrl: PDF_DECODERS,

    ocr: true,
    extractAttachments: true,
    ocrConfig: {
      language: "eng+ukr",
      workerPath: OCR_WORKER,
      corePath: OCR_CORE,
      langPath: OCR_LANG,
      cacheMethod: "write",
      preserveLayout: true,
    },
  });

  const result = await ast.to("md");

  if (typeof result.value !== "string") {
    throw new Error(
      `Парсер повернув неочікуваний формат результату для "${file.name}".`,
    );
  }

  return result.value;
}

async function readImageFile(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const worker = await createWorker("eng+ukr", 1, {
    workerPath: OCR_WORKER,
    corePath: OCR_CORE,
    langPath: OCR_LANG,
    cacheMethod: "write",
  });

  try {
    const result = await worker.recognize(file);
    return result.data.text;
  } finally {
    await worker.terminate();
  }
}

export async function readFile(file: File): Promise<ReadFileResult> {
  const type = getFileType(file);
  let content: string;

  switch (type) {
    case "text":
      content = await readTextFile(file);
      break;

    case "image":
      content = await readImageFile(file);
      break;

    case "pdf":
    case "document":
    case "spreadsheet":
    case "presentation":
    case "epub":
      content = await readOfficeFile(file);
      break;
  }

  if (!content.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      `Не вдалося отримати текст із файлу "${file.name}".`,
    );
  }

  return {
    name: file.name,
    type,
    size: file.size,
    content,
  };
}

export interface StoredAttachmentInput {
  id: string;
  chatId: string;
  fileName: string;
  mimeType: string;
  originalSize: number;
}

export const EXTRACTOR_ID = "sonne-unified";
export const EXTRACTOR_VERSION = "officeparser-8.9.0_tesseract-7.0.0_assets-v2-vision";
const STORED_READ_CHUNK_SIZE = 256 * 1024;
const INDEX_CHUNK_TARGET_TOKENS = 800;
const INDEX_CHUNK_OVERLAP_TOKENS = 120;
const INDEX_CHARS_PER_ESTIMATED_TOKEN = 3;
const INDEX_CHUNK_TARGET_SIZE =
  INDEX_CHUNK_TARGET_TOKENS * INDEX_CHARS_PER_ESTIMATED_TOKEN;
const INDEX_CHUNK_OVERLAP_SIZE =
  INDEX_CHUNK_OVERLAP_TOKENS * INDEX_CHARS_PER_ESTIMATED_TOKEN;
const INDEX_MAX_SECTIONS = 9_000;

type IndexSection = {
  key: string;
  parentKey: string | null;
  sectionType: string;
  title: string | null;
  ordinal: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
};

type IndexChunk = {

  content: string;
  tokenCount: number;
  startOffset: number;
  endOffset: number;
  chunkType: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
};

type ExtractedAsset = {

  assetType: "image" | "chart";
  mimeType: string | null;
  fileName: string | null;
  ordinal: number;
  data: number[];
  metadata: Record<string, unknown> | null;
};

const MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSETS = 1_000;
const MAX_ASSET_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSET_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024;

function isArchiveName(name: string): boolean {
  return /\.(zip|tar|gz|tgz)$/i.test(name);
}

export async function loadStoredAttachment(
  attachment: StoredAttachmentInput,
): Promise<File> {
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(attachment.originalSize) ||
    attachment.originalSize < 0 ||
    attachment.originalSize > 512 * 1024 * 1024
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Некоректний або завеликий розмір вкладення "${attachment.fileName}".`,
    );
  }

  const chunks: ArrayBuffer[] = [];
  let offset = 0;

  while (offset < attachment.originalSize) {
    const length = Math.min(
      STORED_READ_CHUNK_SIZE,
      attachment.originalSize - offset,
    );

    const bytes = await invoke<number[]>("read_attachment_chunk", {
      chatId: attachment.chatId,
      referenceId: attachment.id,
      offset,
      length,
    });

    if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > length) {
      throw new Error(
        `Не вдалося прочитати вкладення "${attachment.fileName}" повністю.`,
      );
    }

    const chunk = Uint8Array.from(bytes);
    chunks.push(
      chunk.buffer.slice(
        chunk.byteOffset,
        chunk.byteOffset + chunk.byteLength,
      ) as ArrayBuffer,
    );

    offset += bytes.length;
  }

  if (offset !== attachment.originalSize) {
    throw new Error(
      `Розмір прочитаного файлу "${attachment.fileName}" не збігається з оригіналом.`,
    );
  }

  return new File(chunks, attachment.fileName, {
    type: attachment.mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
  });
}

function normalizeSectionType(value: unknown): string {
  const type = typeof value === "string" ? value.toLowerCase() : "";

  if (/page/.test(type)) return "page";
  if (/sheet|worksheet/.test(type)) return "sheet";
  if (/slide/.test(type)) return "slide";
  if (/chapter|epub/.test(type)) return "chapter";
  if (/table|row|cell|grid/.test(type)) return "table";
  if (/header/.test(type)) return "header";
  if (/footer/.test(type)) return "footer";
  if (/note|comment/.test(type)) return "notes";
  if (/paragraph|text|heading|title|list|item/.test(type)) {
    return "paragraph";
  }

  return "paragraph";
}

function collectAstSections(ast: unknown): IndexSection[] {
  const sections: IndexSection[] = [{
    key: "root",
    parentKey: null,
    sectionType: "root",
    title: "Документ",
    ordinal: 0,
  }];

  const root = ast as Record<string, unknown>;
  const content = Array.isArray(root?.content) ? root.content : [];
  let ordinal = 1;
  let generatedKey = 0;

  const visit = (
    value: unknown,
    parentKey: string,
    depth: number,
  ): void => {
    if (sections.length >= INDEX_MAX_SECTIONS || depth > 32) return;

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value) {
        visit(item, parentKey, depth + 1);
        if (sections.length >= INDEX_MAX_SECTIONS) break;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return;

    const node = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const rawType = typeof node.type === "string" ? node.type : "paragraph";
    const sectionType = normalizeSectionType(rawType);
    const key = `ast-${generatedKey++}`;

    const rawTitle =
      typeof node.title === "string"
        ? node.title
        : typeof node.text === "string"
          ? node.text
          : "";

    const title = rawTitle.trim().slice(0, 300) || null;

    const metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {
      sourceNodeType: rawType.slice(0, 100),
    };

    if (
      node.metadata &&
      typeof node.metadata === "object" &&
      !Array.isArray(node.metadata)
    ) {
      metadata.sourceMetadata = node.metadata;
    }

    sections.push({
      key,
      parentKey,
      sectionType,
      title,
      ordinal: ordinal++,
      metadata,
    });

    if (node.children !== undefined) {
      visit(node.children, key, depth + 1);
    }
  };

  visit(content, "root", 0);
  return sections;
}

function splitIntoChunks(
  content: string,
  sectionKey = "root",
): IndexChunk[] {
  const normalized = content.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").trim();
  if (!normalized) return [];

  const chunks: IndexChunk[] = [];
  let start = 0;

  const findBoundary = (from: number, hardEnd: number): number => {
    const minimumEnd = from + Math.floor((hardEnd - from) * 0.65);

    const paragraphBreak = normalized.lastIndexOf("\n\n", hardEnd);
    if (paragraphBreak >= minimumEnd) return paragraphBreak + 2;

    const lineBreak = normalized.lastIndexOf("\n", hardEnd);
    if (lineBreak >= minimumEnd) return lineBreak + 1;

    let sentenceBoundary = -1;
    for (const punctuation of [". ", "! ", "? ", "; "]) {
      sentenceBoundary = Math.max(
        sentenceBoundary,
        normalized.lastIndexOf(punctuation, hardEnd),
      );
    }
    if (sentenceBoundary >= minimumEnd) return sentenceBoundary + 1;

    let whitespace = -1;
    for (let i = hardEnd; i >= minimumEnd; i--) {
      if (/\s/.test(normalized[i])) {
        whitespace = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    return whitespace >= minimumEnd ? whitespace : hardEnd;
  };

  while (start < normalized.length) {
    const hardEnd = Math.min(
      start + INDEX_CHUNK_TARGET_SIZE,
      normalized.length,
    );

    const end =
      hardEnd === normalized.length
        ? normalized.length
        : findBoundary(start, hardEnd);

    let contentStart = start;
    let contentEnd = Math.max(end, start + 1);

    while (contentStart < contentEnd && /\s/.test(normalized[contentStart])) {
      contentStart++;
    }
    while (contentEnd > contentStart && /\s/.test(normalized[contentEnd - 1])) {
      contentEnd--;
    }

    const text = normalized.slice(contentStart, contentEnd);

    if (text) {
      chunks.push({
        sectionKey,
        content: text,
        tokenCount: Math.max(
          1,
          Math.ceil(text.length / INDEX_CHARS_PER_ESTIMATED_TOKEN),
        ),
        startOffset: contentStart,
        endOffset: contentEnd,
        chunkType: "text",
      });
    }

    if (end >= normalized.length) break;

    let nextStart = Math.max(
      start + 1,
      contentEnd - INDEX_CHUNK_OVERLAP_SIZE,
    );

    while (nextStart > start && !/\s/.test(normalized[nextStart - 1])) {
      nextStart--;
    }
    while (nextStart < normalized.length && /\s/.test(normalized[nextStart])) {
      nextStart++;
    }

    if (nextStart <= start || nextStart >= end) {
      nextStart = end;
    }

    start = nextStart;
  }

  return chunks;
}

function getAstMetadata(ast: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  const root = ast as Record<string, unknown>;
  const metadata = root?.metadata;

  if (
    metadata &&
    typeof metadata === "object" &&
    !Array.isArray(metadata)
  ) {
    return metadata as Record<string, unknown>;
  }

  return {};
}

function collectAstAssetSectionKeys(ast: unknown): Map<string, string> {
  const result = new Map<string, string>();
  const root = ast as Record<string, unknown>;
  const content = Array.isArray(root?.content) ? root.content : [];

  let generatedKey = 0;
  let visited = 1;

  const visit = (value: unknown, depth: number): void => {
    if (visited >= INDEX_MAX_SECTIONS || depth > 32) return;

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value) {
        visit(item, depth + 1);
        if (visited >= INDEX_MAX_SECTIONS) break;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return;

    const node = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const key = `ast-${generatedKey++}`;
    visited++;

    const metadata =
      node.metadata &&
      typeof node.metadata === "object" &&
      !Array.isArray(node.metadata)
        ? node.metadata as Record<string, unknown>
        : {};

    const attachmentName =
      typeof metadata.attachmentName === "string"
        ? metadata.attachmentName
        : typeof node.attachmentName === "string"
          ? node.attachmentName
          : null;

    if (attachmentName && !result.has(attachmentName)) {
      result.set(attachmentName, key);
    }

    if (node.children !== undefined) {
      visit(node.children, depth + 1);
    }
  };

  visit(content, 0);
  return result;
}

function extractOfficeAssets(ast: unknown): ExtractedAsset[] {
  const root = ast as Record<string, unknown>;
  const attachments = Array.isArray(root?.attachments)
    ? root.attachments
    : [];

  const sectionKeys = collectAstAssetSectionKeys(ast);
  const assets: ExtractedAsset[] = [];
  let totalBytes = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < attachments.length; index++) {
    const raw = attachments[index];

    if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") {
      throw new Error(`Некоректне вкладення OfficeParser №${index + 1}.`);
    }

    const attachment = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
    const assetType = attachment.type;

    if (assetType !== "image" && assetType !== "chart") {
      continue;
    }

    if (assets.length >= MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSETS) {
      throw new Error(
        `Документ містить більше ${MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSETS} зображень і діаграм.`,
      );
    }

    if (typeof attachment.data !== "string" || !attachment.data.trim()) {
      throw new Error(
        `OfficeParser не повернув дані вкладення №${index + 1}.`,
      );
    }

    let base64 = attachment.data.trim();
    let dataUriMime: string | null = null;

    if (base64.startsWith("data:")) {
      const comma = base64.indexOf(",");
      if (comma < 0 || !base64.slice(0, comma).includes(";base64")) {
        throw new Error(`Некоректний Base64 data URI у вкладенні №${index + 1}.`);
      }

      const header = base64.slice(5, comma);
      dataUriMime = header.split(";")[0] || null;
      base64 = base64.slice(comma + 1);
    }

    base64 = base64.replace(/\s/g, "");

    if (
      !base64 ||
      base64.length % 4 !== 0 ||
      !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]*={0,2}$/.test(base64)
    ) {
      throw new Error(`Некоректне Base64-кодування вкладення №${index + 1}.`);
    }

    const estimatedSize =
      (base64.length * 3) / 4 -
      (base64.endsWith("==") ? 2 : base64.endsWith("=") ? 1 : 0);

    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(estimatedSize) ||
      estimatedSize <= 0 ||
      estimatedSize > MAX_ASSET_BYTES
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Вкладення №${index + 1} перевищує ліміт ${MAX_ASSET_BYTES / 1024 / 1024} MiB.`,
      );
    }

    let binary: string;

    try {
      binary = atob(base64);
    } catch {
      throw new Error(`Не вдалося декодувати вкладення №${index + 1}.`);
    }

    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);

    for (let byteIndex = 0; byteIndex < binary.length; byteIndex++) {
      bytes[byteIndex] = binary.charCodeAt(byteIndex);
    }

    totalBytes += bytes.byteLength;

    if (totalBytes > MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSET_BYTES) {
      throw new Error(
        `Сумарний розмір вкладень перевищує ліміт ${MAX_DOCUMENT_ASSET_BYTES / 1024 / 1024} MiB.`,
      );
    }

    const name =
      typeof attachment.name === "string" ? attachment.name : null;

    const mimeType =
      typeof attachment.mimeType === "string"
        ? attachment.mimeType
        : dataUriMime;

    if (name && name.length > 512) {
      throw new Error(`Назва вкладення №${index + 1} задовга.`);
    }

    if (mimeType && mimeType.length > 128) {
      throw new Error(`MIME-тип вкладення №${index + 1} задовгий.`);
    }

    const metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {};

    if (name) metadata.attachmentName = name;

    if (typeof attachment.ocrText === "string" && attachment.ocrText) {
      metadata.ocrText = attachment.ocrText;
    }

    if (typeof attachment.chartData === "object" && attachment.chartData !== null) {
      metadata.chartData = attachment.chartData;
    }

    const astSectionKey = name ? sectionKeys.get(name) ?? null : null;

    if (astSectionKey) metadata.astSectionKey = astSectionKey;

    let metadataJson: string;

    try {
      metadataJson = JSON.stringify(metadata);
    } catch {
      throw new Error(`Не вдалося серіалізувати метадані вкладення №${index + 1}.`);
    }

    if (new TextEncoder().encode(metadataJson).byteLength > 256 * 1024) {
      throw new Error(
        `Метадані вкладення №${index + 1} перевищують ліміт 256 KiB.`,
      );
    }

    assets.push({
      sectionKey: astSectionKey,
      assetType,
      mimeType: mimeType ?? null,
      fileName: name,
      ordinal: index,
      data: Array.from(bytes),
      metadata: Object.keys(metadata).length ? metadata : null,
    });
  }

  return assets;
}

async function extractStoredFile(
  file: File,
): Promise<{
  type: SupportedFileType;
  content: string;
  sections: IndexSection[];
  assets: ExtractedAsset[];
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
}> {
  const type = getFileType(file);

  if (
    type === "pdf" ||
    type === "document" ||
    type === "spreadsheet" ||
    type === "presentation" ||
    type === "epub"
  ) {
    const extension = extensionOf(file.name);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());

    const ast = await OfficeParser.parseOffice(bytes, {
      fileType: (extension === "tsv" ? "csv" : extension) as "pdf" | "docx" | "odt" | "rtf" | "xlsx" | "ods" | "csv" | "pptx" | "odp" | "epub",
      allowRemoteAssets: false,
      pdfWorkerSrc: PDF_WORKER,
      pdfCMapUrl: PDF_CMAPS,
      pdfDecoderUrl: PDF_DECODERS,
      ocr: true,
      extractAttachments: true,
      ocrConfig: {
        language: "eng+ukr",
        workerPath: OCR_WORKER,
        corePath: OCR_CORE,
        langPath: OCR_LANG,
        cacheMethod: "write",
        preserveLayout: true,
      },
    });

    const result = await ast.to("md");
    if (typeof result.value !== "string" || !result.value.trim()) {
      throw new Error(
        `Не вдалося отримати текст із файлу "${file.name}".`,
      );
    }

    const sections = collectAstSections(ast);
    const assets = extractOfficeAssets(ast);

    return {
      type,
      content: result.value,
      sections,
      assets,
      metadata: getAstMetadata(ast),
    };
  }

  const result = await readFile(file);
  if (!result.content.trim()) {
    throw new Error(`Файл "${file.name}" не містить доступного тексту.`);
  }

  return {
    type: result.type,
    content: result.content,
    sections: [{
      key: "root",
      parentKey: null,
      sectionType: "root",
      title: file.name,
      ordinal: 0,
    }],
    assets: [],
    metadata: {
      fileName: file.name,
      mimeType: file.type,
      extraction: result.type === "image" ? "tesseract-ocr" : "plain-text",
    },
  };
}

export async function readStoredAttachment(
  attachment: StoredAttachmentInput,
): Promise<ReadFileResult> {
  const file = await loadStoredAttachment(attachment);
  return readFile(file);
}


function flattenChartData(value: unknown): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let visited = 0;

  const visit = (current: unknown, path: string, depth: number): void => {
    if (visited >= 10000 || depth > 10 || lines.join("\n").length >= 180000) {
      return;
    }

    if (current === null || current === undefined) {
      return;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(current)) {
      current.forEach((item, index) => {
        if (visited < 10000) {
          visit(item, path ? `${path}[${index}]` : `[${index}]`, depth + 1);
        }
      });
      return;
    }

    if (typeof current === "object") {
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(current as Record<string, unknown>)) {
        if (visited >= 10000) break;
        visit(item, path ? `${path}.${key}` : key, depth + 1);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (
      typeof current === "string" ||
      typeof current === "number" ||
      typeof current === "boolean"
    ) {
      const text = String(current).slice(0, 180000);
      lines.push(path ? `${path}: ${text}` : text);
      visited += 1;
    }
  };

  visit(value, "", 0);
  return lines.join("\n").slice(0, 180000);
}

const MAX_BACKGROUND_VISION_IMAGES = 4;
const MAX_BACKGROUND_VISION_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_ASSET_METADATA_BYTES = 256 * 1024;

async function addBackgroundVisionDescriptions(
  assets: ExtractedAsset[],
): Promise<ExtractedAsset[]> {
  const result = [...assets];
  let processedImages = 0;
  let processedBytes = 0;

  for (let index = 0; index < result.length; index += 1) {
    const asset = result[index];
    if (asset.assetType !== "image") continue;
    if (processedImages >= MAX_BACKGROUND_VISION_IMAGES) break;

    const mimeType = asset.mimeType?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
    const fileName = asset.fileName?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
    const supportedImage =
      ["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/webp"].includes(mimeType) ||
      /\.(png|jpe?g|webp)$/.test(fileName);

    if (!supportedImage) continue;

    const imageBytes = Uint8Array.from(asset.data);
    if (imageBytes.byteLength === 0) continue;
    if (processedBytes + imageBytes.byteLength > MAX_BACKGROUND_VISION_BYTES) continue;

    processedImages += 1;
    processedBytes += imageBytes.byteLength;

    try {
      const description = await createVisionDescription(OLLAMA_HOST, imageBytes);
      if (!description) continue;

      const metadata = {
        ...(asset.metadata ?? {}),
        visionDescription: description,
      };
      const metadataSize = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(metadata)).byteLength;

      if (metadataSize > MAX_ASSET_METADATA_BYTES) {
        console.warn(`[Sonne Vision] Метадані зображення №${asset.ordinal + 1} перевищують ліміт.`);
        continue;
      }

      result[index] = { ...asset, metadata };
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(
        `[Sonne Vision] Не вдалося описати зображення №${asset.ordinal + 1}:`,
        error,
      );
    }
  }

  return result;
}

function createAssetIndexChunks(assets: ExtractedAsset[]): IndexChunk[] {
  const result: IndexChunk[] = [];

  for (const asset of assets) {
    const metadata = asset.metadata ?? {};
    const lines: string[] = [];

    lines.push(asset.assetType === "chart" ? "Embedded chart" : "Embedded image");

    if (asset.fileName) {
      lines.push(`File name: ${asset.fileName}`);
    }

    const altText = metadata.altText;
    if (typeof altText === "string" && altText.trim()) {
      lines.push(`Description: ${altText.trim()}`);
    }

    const visionDescription = metadata.visionDescription;
    if (
      asset.assetType === "image" &&
      typeof visionDescription === "string" &&
      visionDescription.trim()
    ) {
      lines.push(`Vision description: ${visionDescription.trim()}`);
    }
    const ocrText = metadata.ocrText;
    if (typeof ocrText === "string" && ocrText.trim()) {
      lines.push(`OCR text:\n${ocrText.trim()}`);
    }

    if (asset.assetType === "chart" && metadata.chartData !== undefined) {
      const chartText = flattenChartData(metadata.chartData);
      if (chartText) {
        lines.push(`Chart data:\n${chartText}`);
      }
    }

    const searchableText = lines.join("\n").trim();
    if (!searchableText) continue;

    const chunks = splitIntoChunks(searchableText, asset.sectionKey ?? "root");

    chunks.forEach((chunk) => {
      result.push({
        ...chunk,
        chunkType: asset.assetType === "chart" ? "chart_data" : "image_ocr",
        metadata: {
          source: "embedded_asset",
          assetType: asset.assetType,
          assetOrdinal: asset.ordinal,
          fileName: asset.fileName,
        },
      });
    });
  }

  return result;
}

export async function processStoredAttachment(
  attachment: StoredAttachmentInput,
): Promise<{ status: "completed" | "skipped"; documentId?: string }> {
  if (isArchiveName(attachment.fileName)) {
    return { status: "skipped" };
  }

  const statusArgs = {
    chatId: attachment.chatId,
    referenceId: attachment.id,
    extractor: EXTRACTOR_ID,
    extractorVersion: EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
  };

  let documentId: string | null = null;
  let stage: "extraction" | "embedding" = "extraction";

  try {
    const existing = await invoke<{
      documentId: string | null;
      status: string | null;
    }>("get_attachment_processing_status", statusArgs);

    documentId =
      existing.status === "completed" ? existing.documentId : null;

    if (!documentId) {
      const started = await invoke<{
        documentId: string | null;
        status: string | null;
      }>("begin_attachment_processing", statusArgs);

      documentId =
        started.status === "completed" ? started.documentId : null;
    }

    if (!documentId) {
      const file = await loadStoredAttachment(attachment);
      const extracted = await extractStoredFile(file);
      const visionAssets = await addBackgroundVisionDescriptions(extracted.assets);
      const chunks = [
        ...splitIntoChunks(extracted.content),
        ...createAssetIndexChunks(visionAssets),
      ];
      if (!chunks.length) {
        throw new Error("Не вдалося отримати текстові фрагменти документа.");
      }

      if (chunks.length > 50000) {
        throw new Error("Документ перевищує ліміт у 50000 фрагментів.");
      }

      const saved = await invoke<{ documentId: string }>(
        "save_extracted_document",
        {
          input: {
            ...statusArgs,
            detectedFormat: extensionOf(attachment.fileName),
            language: "eng+ukr",
            metadata: {
              ...extracted.metadata,
              fileName: attachment.fileName,
              mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
              parserType: "officeparser",
            },
            sourceSize: attachment.originalSize,
            sections: extracted.sections,
            chunks,
            assets: visionAssets,
          },
        },
      );

      documentId = saved.documentId;
    }

    if (!documentId) {
      throw new Error("Не вдалося отримати ID документа.");
    }

    stage = "embedding";

    while (true) {
      const batch = await invoke<
        Array<{ chunkId: string; content: string }>
      >("get_document_embedding_batch", {
        documentId,
        limit: 32,
      });

      if (batch.length === 0) break;

      const vectors = await createEmbeddings(
        OLLAMA_HOST,
        batch.map((chunk) => chunk.content),
      );

      if (
        vectors.length !== batch.length ||
        vectors.some(
          (vector) =>
            vector.length !== 768 ||
            vector.some((value) => !Number.isFinite(value)),
        )
      ) {
        throw new Error("Некоректна відповідь embedding-моделі.");
      }

      await invoke("save_document_embedding_batch", {
        documentId,
        embeddings: batch.map((chunk, index) => ({
          chunkId: chunk.chunkId,
          vector: vectors[index],
        })),
      });
    }

    await invoke("finalize_document_embedding_index", { documentId });

    return { status: "completed", documentId };
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);

    try {
      await invoke("mark_attachment_processing_failed", {
        ...statusArgs,
        errorCode:
          stage === "embedding" ? "embedding_failed" : "extraction_failed",
        errorMessage: message,
      });
    } catch {
      // Preserve the original processing error.
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

import {
  Ollama,
  type AbortableAsyncIterator,
  type ChatRequest,
  type ChatResponse,
  type Fetch,
} from "ollama/browser";

export type OllamaChatStream = AbortableAsyncIterator<ChatResponse>;

export type ActiveOllamaChatStream = {
  stream: OllamaChatStream;
  abort: () => void;
  dispose: () => void;
};

function createAbortableFetch(
  signal: AbortSignal,
  registerCleanup: (cleanup: () => void) => void,
): Fetch {
  return async (input, init = {}) => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const sdkSignal = init.signal;
    let cleaned = false;
    const cleanup = () => {
      if (cleaned) return;

      cleaned = true;
      signal.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
      sdkSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
    };
    const abort = () => {
      cleanup();
      controller.abort();
    };

    signal.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
    sdkSignal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
    registerCleanup(cleanup);

    if (signal.aborted || sdkSignal?.aborted) {
      abort();
    }

    try {
      return await fetch(input, { ...init, signal: controller.signal });
    } catch (error) {
      cleanup();
      throw error;
    }
  };
}

export async function createChatStream(
  host: string,
  request: ChatRequest & { stream: true },
  signal: AbortSignal,
): Promise<ActiveOllamaChatStream> {
  const cleanups: Array<() => void> = [];
  const dispose = () => {
    while (cleanups.length > 0) {
      cleanups.pop()?.();
    }
  };
  const client = new Ollama({
    host,
    fetch: createAbortableFetch(signal, (cleanup) => cleanups.push(cleanup)),
  });

  try {
    const stream = await client.chat(request);

    return {
      stream,
      abort: () => {
        stream.abort();
        dispose();
      },
      dispose,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    dispose();
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function createVisionDescription(
  host: string,
  image: Uint8Array,
): Promise<string> {
  const client = new Ollama({ host });
  const response = await client.chat({
    model: "sonne-gemma:latest",
    stream: false,
    messages: [{
      role: "user",
      content:
        "Опиши це зображення для пошуку в документах. Визнач основні об'єкти, " +
        "схеми, графіки, взаємозв'язки та важливі деталі. Якщо є текст, " +
        "передай лише той, який можна впевнено прочитати. Не вигадуй відсутніх " +
        "даних і не роби припущень. Пиши українською, стисло та інформативно.",
      images: [image],
    }],
    options: { temperature: 0.2, num_predict: 256 },
  });
  return response.message.content.trim();
}

export const OLLAMA_HOST = "http://127.0.0.1:11434";

export async function createEmbeddings(
  host: string,
  input: string[],
): Promise<number[][]> {
  if (input.length === 0) return [];

  const client = new Ollama({ host });
  const response = await client.embed({
    model: "nomic-embed-text:latest",
    input,
    truncate: false,
  });

  return response.embeddings;
}

use std::sync::Mutex;
use tauri::Manager;

mod storage;

const CORE_SYSTEM_RULES: &str = include_str!("../../Core_System_Rules.md");

const SONNE_VOICE: &str = include_str!("../../Sonne_Voice.md");

const PRO_AI_INTELLIGENCE: &str = include_str!("../../Pro_AI_Intelligence.md");

const GEMMA_INTELLIGENCE: &str = include_str!("../../Gemma_Intelligence.md");

const PRO_AI_FINAL: &str = include_str!("../../Pro_AI_Final_Instructions.md");

const GEMMA_FINAL: &str = include_str!("../../Gemma_Final_Instructions.md");

#[tauri::command]
fn greet(name: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "Hello, {}! You've been greeted from the Rust backend!",
        name
    )
}

#[tauri::command]
fn get_runtime_instructions(model: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let (intelligence, final_instructions) = match model {
        "pro-ai" => (PRO_AI_INTELLIGENCE, PRO_AI_FINAL),
        "gemma" => (GEMMA_INTELLIGENCE, GEMMA_FINAL),
        _ => return Err(format!("Unknown model profile: {}", model)),
    };

    Ok([
        CORE_SYSTEM_RULES,
        SONNE_VOICE,
        intelligence,
        final_instructions,
    ]
    .join("\n\n"))
}

#[cfg_attr(mobile, tauri::mobile_entry_point)]
pub fn run() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .register_asynchronous_uri_scheme_protocol("sonne-file", |ctx, request, responder| {
            let app = ctx.app_handle().clone();
            storage::handle_preview_request(app, request, responder);
        })
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_opener::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())
        .setup(|app| {
            let app_data_dir = app.path().app_data_dir()?;

            let storage =
                storage::Storage::initialize(&app_data_dir).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;

            app.manage(Mutex::new(storage));

            Ok(())
        })
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            greet,
            get_runtime_instructions,
            storage::commands::create_chat,
            storage::commands::list_chats,
            storage::commands::get_chat,
            storage::commands::save_message,
            storage::commands::start_turn,
            storage::commands::update_message,
            storage::commands::rewind_chat,
            storage::commands::list_messages,
            storage::commands::delete_chat,
            storage::commands::create_attachment_version,
            storage::commands::add_attachment_reference,
            storage::commands::list_message_attachments,
            storage::commands::list_composer_attachments,
            storage::commands::remove_attachment_reference,
            storage::import::import_attachments,
            storage::import::import_files,
            storage::import::import_clipboard_image,
            storage::attachments::promote_composer_attachments,
            storage::attachments::read_attachment_chunk,
            storage::archive::list_archive_entries,
            storage::archive::read_archive_entry_chunk,
            storage::processing::get_attachment_processing_status,
            storage::processing::begin_attachment_processing,
            storage::processing::save_extracted_document,
            storage::processing::mark_attachment_processing_failed,
            storage::processing::get_attachment_document_context,
            storage::processing::search_attachment_documents,
            storage::processing::get_document_embedding_batch,
            storage::processing::save_document_embedding_batch,
            storage::processing::finalize_document_embedding_index,
            storage::processing::get_document_summary_chunk_batch,
            storage::processing::clear_document_summary_stage,
            storage::processing::save_document_summary_parts,
            storage::processing::get_document_summary_parts,
            storage::processing::finalize_document_summary,
            storage::processing::get_document_summary,
        ])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running tauri application");
}

FILE: src-tauri/src/storage/archive.rs
