use std::sync::Mutex;
use tauri::State;

use super::{ChatRecord, MessageRecord, StartTurnRecord, Storage};

fn lock_storage<'a>(
    state: &'a State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
) -> Result<std::sync::MutexGuard<'a, Storage>, String> {
    state
        .lock()
        .map_err(|_| "Storage lock is poisoned".to_string())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn create_chat(state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>, title: String) -> Result<ChatRecord, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.create_chat(&title)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn list_chats(state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>) -> Result<Vec<ChatRecord>, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.list_chats()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_chat(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
) -> Result<Option<ChatRecord>, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.get_chat(&chat_id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn start_turn(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
    rewind_message_id: Option<String>,
    user_content: String,
    prompt_content: String,
    assistant_content: String,
    assistant_model: Option<String>,
    assistant_mode: Option<String>,
) -> Result<StartTurnRecord, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.start_turn(
        &chat_id,
        rewind_message_id.as_deref(),
        &user_content,
        &prompt_content,
        &assistant_content,
        assistant_model.as_deref(),
        assistant_mode.as_deref(),
    )
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn save_message(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
    role: String,
    content: String,
    prompt_content: Option<String>,
    model_used: Option<String>,
    mode_used: Option<String>,
) -> Result<MessageRecord, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.save_message(
        &chat_id,
        &role,
        &content,
        prompt_content.as_deref(),
        model_used.as_deref(),
        mode_used.as_deref(),
    )
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn list_messages(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
) -> Result<Vec<MessageRecord>, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.list_messages(&chat_id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn delete_chat(state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>, chat_id: String) -> Result<bool, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.delete_chat(&chat_id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn create_attachment_version(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    attachment_id: Option<String>,
    blob_sha256: String,
    file_name: String,
    extension: String,
    mime_type: String,
) -> Result<super::AttachmentVersionRecord, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.create_attachment_version(
        attachment_id.as_deref(),
        &blob_sha256,
        &file_name,
        &extension,
        &mime_type,
    )
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn add_attachment_reference(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
    attachment_version_id: String,
    message_id: Option<String>,
    purpose: String,
    position: i64,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.add_attachment_reference(
        &chat_id,
        &attachment_version_id,
        message_id.as_deref(),
        &purpose,
        position,
    )
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn list_message_attachments(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    message_id: String,
) -> Result<Vec<super::AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.list_message_attachments(&message_id)
}

pub fn list_composer_attachments(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
) -> Result<Vec<super::AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.list_composer_attachments(&chat_id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn remove_attachment_reference(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    reference_id: String,
) -> Result<bool, String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.remove_attachment_reference(&reference_id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn update_message(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    message_id: String,
    content: String,
    prompt_content: Option<String>,
    model_used: Option<String>,
    mode_used: Option<String>,
) -> Result<super::MessageRecord, String> {
    let mut storage = lock_storage(&state)?;
    storage.update_message(
        &message_id,
        &content,
        prompt_content.as_deref(),
        model_used.as_deref(),
        mode_used.as_deref(),
    )
}

pub fn rewind_chat(
    state: State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
    message_id: String,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    lock_storage(&state)?.rewind_chat(&chat_id, &message_id)
}

use std::{
    fs::File,
    io::Cursor,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::Mutex,
    time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};

use rusqlite::params;
use serde_json::json;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager};
use tauri_plugin_dialog::DialogExt;
use uuid::Uuid;

use super::{
    AttachmentReferenceRecord, MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES, MAX_IMPORT_BATCH_SIZE_BYTES, MAX_IMPORT_FILES,
    Storage,
};

fn now_timestamp() -> Result<i64, String> {
    let duration = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_err(|error| format!("System clock error: {error}"))?;

    i64::try_from(duration.as_millis()).map_err(|error| format!("Timestamp overflow: {error}"))
}

fn file_metadata(path: &Path) -> Result<(String, String, String), String> {
    let file_name = path
        .file_name()
        .ok_or_else(|| "Selected file has no filename".to_string())?
        .to_string_lossy()
        .into_owned();

    let extension = path
        .extension()
        .and_then(|value| value.to_str())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();

    let mime_type = match extension.as_str() {
        "pdf" => "application/pdf",
        "png" => "image/png",
        "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
        "webp" => "image/webp",
        "gif" => "image/gif",
        "bmp" => "image/bmp",
        "tif" | "tiff" => "image/tiff",
        "avif" => "image/avif",
        "heic" | "heif" => "image/heic",
        "txt" | "log" | "csv" | "tsv" => "text/plain",
        "md" | "markdown" => "text/markdown",
        "html" | "htm" => "text/html",
        "json" => "application/json",
        "xml" => "application/xml",
        "js" | "mjs" | "cjs" => "text/javascript",
        "ts" | "tsx" => "text/typescript",
        "css" => "text/css",
        "rs" => "text/rust",
        "py" => "text/x-python",
        "java" => "text/x-java-source",
        "c" | "h" => "text/x-c",
        "cpp" | "hpp" | "cc" => "text/x-c++",
        "sh" | "bash" => "text/x-shellscript",
        "yaml" | "yml" => "application/yaml",
        "toml" => "application/toml",
        "docx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
        "pptx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
        "xlsx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
        "odt" => "application/vnd.oasis.opendocument.text",
        "ods" => "application/vnd.oasis.opendocument.spreadsheet",
        "odp" => "application/vnd.oasis.opendocument.presentation",
        "epub" => "application/epub+zip",
        "zip" => "application/zip",
        "gz" => "application/gzip",
        "tar" => "application/x-tar",
        "rtf" => "application/rtf",
        _ => "application/octet-stream",
    };

    Ok((file_name, extension, mime_type.to_string()))
}

struct PreparedAttachment {
    blob_sha256: String,
    file_name: String,
    extension: String,
    mime_type: String,
}

impl Storage {
    fn import_selected_paths(
        &mut self,
        chat_id: &str,
        paths: Vec<PathBuf>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
        if paths.is_empty() {
            return self.list_composer_attachments(chat_id);
        }

        if paths.len() > MAX_IMPORT_FILES {
            return Err(format!(
                "An import batch cannot contain more than {MAX_IMPORT_FILES} files"
            ));
        }

        let mut declared_batch_size = 0u64;

        for path in &paths {
            let metadata = std::fs::metadata(path)
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot inspect file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;

            if !metadata.is_file() {
                return Err(format!("Not a regular file: {}", path.display()));
            }

            let file_size = metadata.len();

            if file_size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES {
                return Err(format!(
                    "File {} exceeds the maximum size of {} MiB",
                    path.display(),
                    MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)
                ));
            }

            declared_batch_size = declared_batch_size
                .checked_add(file_size)
                .ok_or_else(|| "Import batch size overflow".to_string())?;

            if declared_batch_size > MAX_IMPORT_BATCH_SIZE_BYTES {
                return Err(format!(
                    "The total import batch exceeds the maximum size of {} MiB",
                    MAX_IMPORT_BATCH_SIZE_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)
                ));
            }
        }

        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;
        let operation_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();

        let chat_exists: bool = self
            .connection
            .query_row(
                "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM chats WHERE id = ?1)",
                params![chat_id],
                |row| row.get(0),
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot verify import chat: {error}"))?;

        if !chat_exists {
            return Err(format!("Chat not found: {chat_id}"));
        }

        let existing = self.list_composer_attachments(chat_id)?;
        let mut position = i64::try_from(existing.len())
            .map_err(|error| format!("Attachment position overflow: {error}"))?;

        self.connection
            .execute(
                "INSERT INTO storage_operations (
                    id, operation_type, state, metadata_json,
                    created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES (?1, 'import_batch', 'PENDING', ?2, ?3, ?3)",
                params![
                    operation_id,
                    json!({
                        "chatId": chat_id,
                        "files": []
                    })
                    .to_string(),
                    timestamp
                ],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot create import journal: {error}"))?;

        self.connection
            .execute(
                "UPDATE storage_operations
                 SET state = 'PROCESSING', updated_at = ?1
                 WHERE id = ?2",
                params![now_timestamp()?, operation_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start import journal: {error}"))?;

        let preparation = (|| -> Result<Vec<PreparedAttachment>, String> {
            let mut prepared = Vec::with_capacity(paths.len());
            let mut imported_size = 0u64;

            for path in paths {
                let metadata = std::fs::metadata(&path)
                    .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot inspect file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;

                if !metadata.is_file() {
                    return Err(format!("Not a regular file: {}", path.display()));
                }

                let (file_name, extension, mime_type) = file_metadata(&path)?;

                let file = File::open(&path)
                    .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot open {file_name}: {error}"))?;

                let remaining_batch_size =
                    MAX_IMPORT_BATCH_SIZE_BYTES.saturating_sub(imported_size);
                let effective_limit = MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES.min(remaining_batch_size);
                let blob = self.store_blob_with_limit(file, effective_limit)?;

                imported_size = imported_size
                    .checked_add(
                        u64::try_from(blob.original_size)
                            .map_err(|error| format!("Invalid stored blob size: {error}"))?,
                    )
                    .ok_or_else(|| "Import batch size overflow".to_string())?;

                prepared.push(PreparedAttachment {
                    blob_sha256: blob.sha256,
                    file_name,
                    extension,
                    mime_type,
                });

                let files_json: Vec<_> = prepared
                    .iter()
                    .map(|item| {
                        json!({
                            "blobSha256": item.blob_sha256,
                            "fileName": item.file_name,
                            "extension": item.extension,
                            "mimeType": item.mime_type
                        })
                    })
                    .collect();

                self.connection
                    .execute(
                        "UPDATE storage_operations
                         SET metadata_json = ?1, updated_at = ?2
                         WHERE id = ?3",
                        params![
                            json!({
                                "chatId": chat_id,
                                "files": files_json
                            })
                            .to_string(),
                            now_timestamp()?,
                            operation_id
                        ],
                    )
                    .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update import journal: {error}"))?;
            }

            Ok(prepared)
        })();

        let prepared = match preparation {
            Ok(prepared) => prepared,
            Err(error) => {
                let _ = self.connection.execute(
                    "UPDATE storage_operations
                     SET state = 'FAILED', error_message = ?1, updated_at = ?2
                     WHERE id = ?3",
                    params![error, now_timestamp().unwrap_or(timestamp), operation_id],
                );
                return Err(error);
            }
        };

        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start atomic attachment transaction: {error}"))?;

        let commit_result = (|| -> Result<(), String> {
            for item in &prepared {
                let version = super::attachments::create_attachment_version_in_transaction(
                    &transaction,
                    None,
                    &item.blob_sha256,
                    &item.file_name,
                    &item.extension,
                    &item.mime_type,
                    timestamp,
                )?;

                super::attachments::add_attachment_reference_in_transaction(
                    &transaction,
                    chat_id,
                    &version.id,
                    None,
                    "composer",
                    position,
                    timestamp,
                )?;

                position = position
                    .checked_add(1)
                    .ok_or_else(|| "Attachment position overflow".to_string())?;
            }

            transaction
                .execute(
                    "UPDATE storage_operations
                     SET state = 'COMPLETED', updated_at = ?1, error_message = NULL
                     WHERE id = ?2",
                    params![now_timestamp()?, operation_id],
                )
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot complete import journal: {error}"))?;

            Ok(())
        })();

        if let Err(error) = commit_result {
            drop(transaction);

            let _ = self.connection.execute(
                "UPDATE storage_operations
                 SET state = 'FAILED', error_message = ?1, updated_at = ?2
                 WHERE id = ?3",
                params![error, now_timestamp().unwrap_or(timestamp), operation_id],
            );

            return Err(error);
        }

        transaction
            .commit()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot commit imported attachments: {error}"))?;

        for item in &prepared {
            if super::archive::is_supported_archive_extension(&item.extension, &item.file_name) {
                if let Err(error) = super::archive::index_imported_archive(
                    self,
                    &item.blob_sha256,
                    &item.file_name,
                    &item.extension,
                ) {
                    eprintln!("Archive indexing failed for {}: {}", item.file_name, error);
                }
            }
        }

        self.list_composer_attachments(chat_id)
    }
}

impl Storage {
    fn import_clipboard_image(
        &mut self,
        chat_id: &str,
        requested_mime: &str,
        data: Vec<u8>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
        if data.is_empty() {
            return Err("Clipboard image is empty".to_string());
        }

        let data_size = u64::try_from(data.len())
            .map_err(|error| format!("Invalid clipboard image size: {error}"))?;

        if data_size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES {
            return Err(format!(
                "Clipboard image exceeds the maximum size of {} MiB",
                MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)
            ));
        }

        let (extension, mime_type) = match requested_mime.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "image/png" => ("png", "image/png"),
            "image/jpeg" => ("jpg", "image/jpeg"),
            "image/webp" => ("webp", "image/webp"),
            "image/gif" => ("gif", "image/gif"),
            "image/bmp" => ("bmp", "image/bmp"),
            "image/tiff" => ("tif", "image/tiff"),
            "image/avif" => ("avif", "image/avif"),
            "image/heic" => ("heic", "image/heic"),
            "image/heif" => ("heif", "image/heif"),
            _ => return Err("Unsupported clipboard image format".to_string()),
        };

        let chat_exists: bool = self
            .connection
            .query_row(
                "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM chats WHERE id = ?1)",
                params![chat_id],
                |row| row.get(0),
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot verify clipboard target chat: {error}"))?;

        if !chat_exists {
            return Err(format!("Chat not found: {chat_id}"));
        }

        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;
        let file_name = format!("clipboard-{timestamp}.{extension}");
        let existing = self.list_composer_attachments(chat_id)?;
        let position = i64::try_from(existing.len())
            .map_err(|error| format!("Attachment position overflow: {error}"))?;

        let blob = self.store_blob_with_limit(Cursor::new(data), MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES)?;

        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start clipboard attachment transaction: {error}"))?;

        let result = (|| -> Result<(), String> {
            let version = super::attachments::create_attachment_version_in_transaction(
                &transaction,
                None,
                &blob.sha256,
                &file_name,
                extension,
                mime_type,
                timestamp,
            )?;

            super::attachments::add_attachment_reference_in_transaction(
                &transaction,
                chat_id,
                &version.id,
                None,
                "composer",
                position,
                timestamp,
            )?;

            Ok(())
        })();

        if let Err(error) = result {
            drop(transaction);
            let _ = self.delete_unreferenced_blob(&blob.sha256);
            return Err(error);
        }

        if let Err(error) = transaction.commit() {
            let _ = self.delete_unreferenced_blob(&blob.sha256);
            return Err(format!("Cannot commit clipboard image attachment: {error}"));
        }

        self.list_composer_attachments(chat_id)
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn import_clipboard_image(
    state: tauri::State<'_, Mutex<Storage>>,
    chat_id: String,
    mime_type: String,
    data: Vec<u8>,
) -> Result<Vec<AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
    let mut storage = state
        .lock()
        .map_err(|error| format!("Storage lock poisoned: {error}"))?;

    storage.import_clipboard_image(&chat_id, &mime_type, data)
}

fn process_selected_paths(app: AppHandle, paths: Vec<PathBuf>, chat_id: String) {
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let result = (|| -> Result<Vec<AttachmentReferenceRecord>, String> {
            let state = app.state::<Mutex<Storage>>();
            let mut storage = state
                .lock()
                .map_err(|error| format!("Storage lock poisoned: {error}"))?;

            storage.import_selected_paths(&chat_id, paths)
        })();

        match result {
            Ok(attachments) => {
                let _ = app.emit("attachments-imported", attachments);
            }
            Err(error) => {
                // Keep the frontend draft synchronized even after a failed batch.
                let current = (|| {
                    let state = app.state::<Mutex<Storage>>();
                    let storage = state.lock().ok()?;
                    storage.list_composer_attachments(&chat_id).ok()
                })();

                if let Some(attachments) = current {
                    let _ = app.emit("attachments-imported", attachments);
                }

                let _ = app.emit("attachments-import-error", error);
            }
        }
    });
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn import_attachments(app: AppHandle, chat_id: String) -> Result<(), String> {
    let callback_app = app.clone();

    app.dialog().file().pick_files(move |selected_files| {
        if let Some(selected_files) = selected_files {
            let paths = selected_files
                .into_iter()
                .map(|file| file.into_path())
                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>();

            match paths {
                Ok(paths) => process_selected_paths(callback_app, paths, chat_id),
                Err(error) => {
                    let _ = callback_app.emit(
                        "attachments-import-error",
                        format!("Cannot resolve selected files: {error}"),
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    });

    Ok(())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn import_files(app: AppHandle, chat_id: String, paths: Vec<String>) -> Result<(), String> {
    if paths.is_empty() {
        return Ok(());
    }

    process_selected_paths(app, paths.into_iter().map(PathBuf::from).collect(), chat_id);

    Ok(())
}

pub mod archive;
pub mod processing;
mod schema;

use std::{
    fs,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::OnceLock,
    time::Duration,
};

use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags};
use schema::{INITIAL_SCHEMA, MIGRATION_1_TO_2, MIGRATION_2_TO_3, MIGRATION_3_TO_4, SCHEMA_VERSION};

pub(super) const MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES: u64 = 512 * 1024 * 1024;
pub(super) const MAX_IMPORT_BATCH_SIZE_BYTES: u64 = 1024 * 1024 * 1024;
pub(super) const MAX_IMPORT_FILES: usize = 100;

pub struct Storage {
    connection: Connection,
    root_dir: PathBuf,
}

fn register_sqlite_vec() -> Result<(), String> {
    static REGISTRATION: OnceLock<Result<(), i32>> = OnceLock::new();

    REGISTRATION
        .get_or_init(|| unsafe {
            let result = rusqlite::ffi::sqlite3_auto_extension(Some(std::mem::transmute(
                sqlite_vec::sqlite3_vec_init as *const (),
            )));

            if result == rusqlite::ffi::SQLITE_OK {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                Err(result)
            }
        })
        .as_ref()
        .map(|_| ())
        .map_err(|code| format!("Cannot register sqlite-vec extension: SQLite error {code}"))
}

impl Storage {
    pub fn initialize(app_data_dir: &Path) -> Result<Self, String> {
        register_sqlite_vec()?;
        let root_dir = app_data_dir.join("storage");

        for directory in [
            root_dir.join("blobs"),
            root_dir.join("temp"),
            root_dir.join("extracted"),
            root_dir.join("indexes"),
        ] {
            fs::create_dir_all(&directory)
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot create storage directory: {error}"))?;
        }

        let database_path = root_dir.join("sonne.db");

        let mut connection = Connection::open_with_flags(
            &database_path,
            OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_WRITE
                | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE
                | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_FULL_MUTEX,
        )
        .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot open Sonne database: {error}"))?;

        let _: String = connection
            .query_row("SELECT vec_version()", [], |row| row.get(0))
            .map_err(|error| format!("sqlite-vec extension is unavailable: {error}"))?;

        connection
            .busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot configure database timeout: {error}"))?;

        connection
            .execute_batch(
                "
                PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
                PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
                PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;
                PRAGMA temp_store = MEMORY;
                PRAGMA mmap_size = 268435456;
                ",
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot configure SQLite: {error}"))?;

        let current_version: i64 = connection
            .pragma_query_value(None, "user_version", |row| row.get(0))
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot read database version: {error}"))?;

        if current_version > SCHEMA_VERSION {
            return Err(format!(
                "Database version {current_version} is newer than supported version {SCHEMA_VERSION}"
            ));
        }

        if current_version < SCHEMA_VERSION {
            let transaction = connection
                .transaction()
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot begin schema migration: {error}"))?;

            match current_version {
                0 => {
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(INITIAL_SCHEMA)
                        .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot initialize database schema: {error}"))?;

                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_1_TO_2)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 1 to 2: {error}")
                        })?;
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_2_TO_3)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 2 to 3: {error}")
                        })?;
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_3_TO_4)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 3 to 4: {error}")
                        })?;
                }
                1 => {
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_1_TO_2)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 1 to 2: {error}")
                        })?;
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_2_TO_3)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 2 to 3: {error}")
                        })?;
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_3_TO_4)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 3 to 4: {error}")
                        })?;
                }
                2 => {
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_2_TO_3)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 2 to 3: {error}")
                        })?;
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_3_TO_4)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 3 to 4: {error}")
                        })?;
                }
                3 => {
                    transaction
                        .execute_batch(MIGRATION_3_TO_4)
                        .map_err(|error| {
                            format!("Cannot migrate database schema 3 to 4: {error}")
                        })?;
                }
                version => {
                    return Err(format!("No migration path from database version {version}"));
                }
            }

            transaction
                .pragma_update(None, "user_version", SCHEMA_VERSION)
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update database version: {error}"))?;

            transaction
                .commit()
                .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot commit schema migration: {error}"))?;
        }

        blobs::recover_interrupted_operations(&connection, &root_dir)?;
        processing::recover_interrupted_document_processing(&connection)?;
        Ok(Self {
            connection,
            root_dir,
        })
    }

    pub fn connection(&self) -> &Connection {
        &self.connection
    }

    pub fn root_dir(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.root_dir
    }
}

pub use records::{ChatRecord, MessageRecord, StartTurnRecord};

pub use blobs::BlobRecord;

pub mod attachments;
pub use attachments::{AttachmentReferenceRecord, AttachmentVersionRecord};
