use rusqlite::{OptionalExtension, params};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use uuid::Uuid;

use super::Storage;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ChatRecord {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub updated_at: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct MessageRecord {
    pub id: String,
    pub chat_id: String,
    pub role: String,
    pub content: String,
    pub prompt_content: Option<String>,
    pub model_used: Option<String>,
    pub mode_used: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct StartTurnRecord {
    pub user_message: MessageRecord,
    pub assistant_message: MessageRecord,
    pub attachments: Vec<super::AttachmentReferenceRecord>,
}

fn now_timestamp() -> Result<i64, String> {
    let duration = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_err(|error| format!("System clock error: {error}"))?;

    i64::try_from(duration.as_millis()).map_err(|error| format!("Timestamp overflow: {error}"))
}

impl Storage {
    pub fn create_chat(&mut self, title: &str) -> Result<ChatRecord, String> {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;

        self.connection
            .execute(
                "INSERT INTO chats (id, title, created_at, updated_at)
                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?3)",
                params![id, title, timestamp],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot create chat: {error}"))?;

        Ok(ChatRecord {
            id,
            title: title.to_owned(),
            created_at: timestamp,
            updated_at: timestamp,
        })
    }

    pub fn get_chat(&self, chat_id: &str) -> Result<Option<ChatRecord>, String> {
        self.connection
            .query_row(
                "SELECT id, title, created_at, updated_at
                 FROM chats WHERE id = ?1",
                params![chat_id],
                |row| {
                    Ok(ChatRecord {
                        id: row.get(0)?,
                        title: row.get(1)?,
                        created_at: row.get(2)?,
                        updated_at: row.get(3)?,
                    })
                },
            )
            .optional()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot retrieve chat: {error}"))
    }

    pub fn list_chats(&self) -> Result<Vec<ChatRecord>, String> {
        let mut statement = self
            .connection
            .prepare(
                "SELECT id, title, created_at, updated_at
                 FROM chats
                 ORDER BY updated_at DESC",
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot prepare chat query: {error}"))?;

        let rows = statement
            .query_map([], |row| {
                Ok(ChatRecord {
                    id: row.get(0)?,
                    title: row.get(1)?,
                    created_at: row.get(2)?,
                    updated_at: row.get(3)?,
                })
            })
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot list chats: {error}"))?;

        rows.collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot read chat records: {error}"))
    }

    pub fn start_turn(
        &mut self,
        chat_id: &str,
        rewind_message_id: Option<&str>,
        user_content: &str,
        prompt_content: &str,
        assistant_content: &str,
        assistant_model: Option<&str>,
        assistant_mode: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<StartTurnRecord, String> {
        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;

        let user_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let assistant_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();

        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot start turn transaction: {e}"))?;

        let chat_exists: bool = transaction
            .query_row(
                "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM chats WHERE id = ?1)",
                params![chat_id],
                |row| row.get(0),
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot verify chat: {e}"))?;

        if !chat_exists {
            return Err(format!("Chat not found: {chat_id}"));
        }

        if let Some(message_id) = rewind_message_id {
            let (target_rowid, role): (i64, String) = transaction
                .query_row(
                    "SELECT rowid, role FROM messages WHERE id = ?1 AND chat_id = ?2",
                    params![message_id, chat_id],
                    |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot find message to edit: {e}"))?;

            if role != "user" {
                return Err("Only user messages can be edited".to_string());
            }

            transaction
                .execute(
                    "UPDATE attachment_references
                 SET message_id = NULL, purpose = 'composer'
                 WHERE message_id = ?1 AND chat_id = ?2",
                    params![message_id, chat_id],
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot restore edited attachments: {e}"))?;

            transaction
                .execute(
                    "DELETE FROM messages WHERE chat_id = ?1 AND rowid >= ?2",
                    params![chat_id, target_rowid],
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot truncate conversation: {e}"))?;
        }

        transaction
            .execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (
                id, chat_id, role, content, prompt_content,
                model_used, mode_used, created_at
            ) VALUES (?1, ?2, 'user', ?3, ?4, NULL, NULL, ?5)",
                params![user_id, chat_id, user_content, prompt_content, timestamp],
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot save user message: {e}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE attachment_references
             SET message_id = ?1, purpose = 'message'
             WHERE chat_id = ?2 AND purpose = 'composer'",
                params![user_id, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot promote attachments: {e}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (
                id, chat_id, role, content, prompt_content,
                model_used, mode_used, created_at
            ) VALUES (?1, ?2, 'assistant', ?3, NULL, ?4, ?5, ?6)",
                params![
                    assistant_id,
                    chat_id,
                    assistant_content,
                    assistant_model,
                    assistant_mode,
                    timestamp
                ],
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot create assistant message: {e}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE chats SET updated_at = ?1 WHERE id = ?2",
                params![timestamp, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot update chat timestamp: {e}"))?;

        let attachments = {
            let mut statement = transaction
                .prepare(
                    "SELECT
                    r.id,
                    r.chat_id,
                    r.message_id,
                    r.attachment_version_id,
                    v.attachment_id,
                    v.version_number,
                    v.file_name,
                    v.extension,
                    v.mime_type,
                    v.blob_sha256,
                    b.original_size,
                    r.position,
                    r.purpose,
                    r.created_at
                 FROM attachment_references r
                 JOIN attachment_versions v
                    ON v.id = r.attachment_version_id
                 JOIN blobs b
                    ON b.sha256 = v.blob_sha256
                 WHERE r.message_id = ?1
                 ORDER BY r.position ASC, r.created_at ASC",
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot prepare turn attachment query: {e}"))?;

            let rows = statement
                .query_map(params![user_id], |row| {
                    Ok(super::AttachmentReferenceRecord {
                        id: row.get(0)?,
                        chat_id: row.get(1)?,
                        message_id: row.get(2)?,
                        attachment_version_id: row.get(3)?,
                        attachment_id: row.get(4)?,
                        version_number: row.get(5)?,
                        file_name: row.get(6)?,
                        extension: row.get(7)?,
                        mime_type: row.get(8)?,
                        blob_sha256: row.get(9)?,
                        original_size: row.get(10)?,
                        position: row.get(11)?,
                        purpose: row.get(12)?,
                        created_at: row.get(13)?,
                    })
                })
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot query turn attachments: {e}"))?;

            rows.collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot read turn attachments: {e}"))?
        };

        transaction
            .commit()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot commit turn: {e}"))?;

        Ok(StartTurnRecord {
            user_message: MessageRecord {
                id: user_id,
                chat_id: chat_id.to_owned(),
                role: "user".to_string(),
                content: user_content.to_owned(),
                prompt_content: Some(prompt_content.to_owned()),
                model_used: None,
                mode_used: None,
                created_at: timestamp,
            },
            assistant_message: MessageRecord {
                id: assistant_id,
                chat_id: chat_id.to_owned(),
                role: "assistant".to_string(),
                content: assistant_content.to_owned(),
                prompt_content: None,
                model_used: assistant_model.map(str::to_owned),
                mode_used: assistant_mode.map(str::to_owned),
                created_at: timestamp,
            },
            attachments,
        })
    }

    pub fn save_message(
        &mut self,
        chat_id: &str,
        role: &str,
        content: &str,
        prompt_content: Option<&str>,
        model_used: Option<&str>,
        mode_used: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<MessageRecord, String> {
        if !matches!(role, "user" | "assistant" | "system") {
            return Err(format!("Unsupported message role: {role}"));
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;

        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start message transaction: {error}"))?;

        let chat_exists: bool = transaction
            .query_row(
                "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM chats WHERE id = ?1)",
                params![chat_id],
                |row| row.get(0),
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot verify chat: {error}"))?;

        if !chat_exists {
            return Err(format!("Chat not found: {chat_id}"));
        }

        transaction
            .execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (
                    id, chat_id, role, content, prompt_content,
                    model_used, mode_used, created_at
                 ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)",
                params![
                    id,
                    chat_id,
                    role,
                    content,
                    prompt_content,
                    model_used,
                    mode_used,
                    timestamp
                ],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot save message: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE chats SET updated_at = ?1 WHERE id = ?2",
                params![timestamp, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update chat timestamp: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .commit()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot commit message transaction: {error}"))?;

        Ok(MessageRecord {
            id,
            chat_id: chat_id.to_owned(),
            role: role.to_owned(),
            content: content.to_owned(),
            prompt_content: prompt_content.map(str::to_owned),
            model_used: model_used.map(str::to_owned),
            mode_used: mode_used.map(str::to_owned),
            created_at: timestamp,
        })
    }

    pub fn list_messages(&self, chat_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<MessageRecord>, String> {
        let mut statement = self
            .connection
            .prepare(
                "SELECT id, chat_id, role, content, prompt_content,
                        model_used, mode_used, created_at
                 FROM messages
                 WHERE chat_id = ?1
                 ORDER BY created_at ASC, rowid ASC",
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot prepare message query: {error}"))?;

        let rows = statement
            .query_map(params![chat_id], |row| {
                Ok(MessageRecord {
                    id: row.get(0)?,
                    chat_id: row.get(1)?,
                    role: row.get(2)?,
                    content: row.get(3)?,
                    prompt_content: row.get(4)?,
                    model_used: row.get(5)?,
                    mode_used: row.get(6)?,
                    created_at: row.get(7)?,
                })
            })
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot list messages: {error}"))?;

        rows.collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot read message records: {error}"))
    }

    pub fn update_message(
        &mut self,
        message_id: &str,
        content: &str,
        prompt_content: Option<&str>,
        model_used: Option<&str>,
        mode_used: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<MessageRecord, String> {
        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;
        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start message update: {error}"))?;

        let chat_id: String = transaction
            .query_row(
                "SELECT chat_id FROM messages WHERE id = ?1",
                params![message_id],
                |row| row.get(0),
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot find message: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE messages
             SET content = ?1, prompt_content = ?2, model_used = ?3, mode_used = ?4
             WHERE id = ?5",
                params![content, prompt_content, model_used, mode_used, message_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update message: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE chats SET updated_at = ?1 WHERE id = ?2",
                params![timestamp, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update chat timestamp: {error}"))?;

        let message = transaction
            .query_row(
                "SELECT id, chat_id, role, content, prompt_content,
                    model_used, mode_used, created_at
             FROM messages WHERE id = ?1",
                params![message_id],
                |row| {
                    Ok(MessageRecord {
                        id: row.get(0)?,
                        chat_id: row.get(1)?,
                        role: row.get(2)?,
                        content: row.get(3)?,
                        prompt_content: row.get(4)?,
                        model_used: row.get(5)?,
                        mode_used: row.get(6)?,
                        created_at: row.get(7)?,
                    })
                },
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot retrieve updated message: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .commit()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot commit message update: {error}"))?;

        Ok(message)
    }

    pub fn rewind_chat(&mut self, chat_id: &str, message_id: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let timestamp = now_timestamp()?;
        let transaction = self
            .connection
            .transaction()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot start chat rewind: {error}"))?;

        let (target_rowid, role): (i64, String) = transaction
            .query_row(
                "SELECT rowid, role FROM messages WHERE id = ?1 AND chat_id = ?2",
                params![message_id, chat_id],
                |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)),
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot find message in chat: {error}"))?;

        if role != "user" {
            return Err("Only user messages can be edited".to_string());
        }

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE attachment_references
             SET message_id = NULL, purpose = 'composer'
             WHERE message_id = ?1 AND chat_id = ?2",
                params![message_id, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot restore message attachments: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "DELETE FROM messages WHERE chat_id = ?1 AND rowid >= ?2",
                params![chat_id, target_rowid],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot remove later messages: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .execute(
                "UPDATE chats SET updated_at = ?1 WHERE id = ?2",
                params![timestamp, chat_id],
            )
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot update chat timestamp: {error}"))?;

        transaction
            .commit()
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot commit chat rewind: {error}"))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn delete_chat(&mut self, chat_id: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let deleted = self
            .connection
            .execute("DELETE FROM chats WHERE id = ?1", params![chat_id])
            .map_err(|error| format!("Cannot delete chat: {error}"))?;

        Ok(deleted > 0)
    }
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: src-tauri/src/storage/schema.rs
====================================================================================================
pub const SCHEMA_VERSION: i64 = 4;

pub const INITIAL_SCHEMA: &str = r#"
PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chats (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    title TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    chat_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('user', 'assistant', 'system')),
    content TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    prompt_content TEXT,
    model_used TEXT,
    mode_used TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (chat_id) REFERENCES chats(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_chat_created
ON messages(chat_id, created_at);

-- One physical file per SHA-256 hash.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blobs (
    sha256 TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    relative_path TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    original_size INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (original_size >= 0),
    stored_size INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (stored_size >= 0),
    storage_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'original'
        CHECK (storage_state IN ('original', 'compressed', 'processing')),
    compression_format TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    last_accessed_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    verified_at INTEGER
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachments (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachment_versions (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    attachment_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    version_number INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (version_number > 0),
    blob_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    extension TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    mime_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'application/octet-stream',
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (attachment_id, version_number),
    FOREIGN KEY (attachment_id) REFERENCES attachments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (blob_sha256) REFERENCES blobs(sha256) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachment_versions_blob
ON attachment_versions(blob_sha256);

-- A reference may belong to a composer draft or a sent message.
-- Chat ownership allows safe cleanup when a chat is permanently deleted.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachment_references (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    chat_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    attachment_version_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    message_id TEXT,
    purpose TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (purpose IN ('composer', 'message')),
    position INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (chat_id) REFERENCES chats(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (message_id) REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (attachment_version_id)
        REFERENCES attachment_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachment_refs_chat
ON attachment_references(chat_id, purpose, position);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachment_refs_version
ON attachment_references(attachment_version_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachment_refs_message
ON attachment_references(message_id);

-- Extraction results are reusable for identical physical files.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS extracted_documents (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    blob_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
    extractor TEXT NOT NULL,
    extractor_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'processing', 'completed', 'failed')),
    language TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (blob_sha256, extractor, extractor_version),
    FOREIGN KEY (blob_sha256) REFERENCES blobs(sha256) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_chunks (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ordinal >= 0),
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    token_count INTEGER,
    start_offset INTEGER,
    end_offset INTEGER,
    UNIQUE (document_id, ordinal),
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_chunks_document
ON document_chunks(document_id, ordinal);

-- Embeddings are versioned by model name; vectors are stored as float32 BLOBs.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS embeddings (
    chunk_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    dimensions INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (dimensions > 0),
    vector BLOB NOT NULL,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (chunk_id, model_name),
    FOREIGN KEY (chunk_id) REFERENCES document_chunks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- Full-text search is kept in sync with document_chunks.
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chunk_search USING fts5(
    chunk_id UNINDEXED,
    content,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'
);

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_chunks_ai
AFTER INSERT ON document_chunks
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO chunk_search(chunk_id, content)
    VALUES (new.id, new.content);
END;

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_chunks_ad
AFTER DELETE ON document_chunks
BEGIN
    DELETE FROM chunk_search WHERE chunk_id = old.id;
END;

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_chunks_au
AFTER UPDATE OF content ON document_chunks
BEGIN
    DELETE FROM chunk_search WHERE chunk_id = old.id;
    INSERT INTO chunk_search(chunk_id, content)
    VALUES (new.id, new.content);
END;

-- Crash-safe journal for filesystem operations.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS storage_operations (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    operation_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    blob_sha256 TEXT,
    source_path TEXT,
    target_path TEXT,
    state TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (state IN (
            'PENDING',
            'PROCESSING',
            'VERIFYING',
            'COMMITTING',
            'COMPLETED',
            'FAILED'
        )),
    metadata_json TEXT,
    error_message TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (blob_sha256) REFERENCES blobs(sha256) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_storage_operations_state
ON storage_operations(state, updated_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS storage_metadata (
    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value TEXT NOT NULL
);

INSERT OR IGNORE INTO storage_metadata(key, value)
VALUES ('schema_version', '1');
"#;

pub const MIGRATION_2_TO_3: &str = r#"
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_vectors_nomic USING vec0(
    chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    embedding float[768] distance_metric=cosine
);
"#;

pub const MIGRATION_1_TO_2: &str = r#"
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN detected_format TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN metadata_json TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN content_relative_path TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN preview_relative_path TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN error_code TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN error_message TEXT;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN processed_at INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE extracted_documents ADD COLUMN source_size INTEGER;

CREATE TABLE document_sections (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    parent_id TEXT,
    section_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        section_type IN (
            'root', 'page', 'sheet', 'slide', 'chapter', 'table',
            'paragraph', 'header', 'footer', 'notes', 'archive_entry'
        )
    ),
    title TEXT,
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    metadata_json TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES document_sections(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX idx_document_sections_document
ON document_sections(document_id, ordinal);

CREATE INDEX idx_document_sections_parent
ON document_sections(parent_id, ordinal);

ALTER TABLE document_chunks ADD COLUMN section_id TEXT REFERENCES document_sections(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
ALTER TABLE document_chunks ADD COLUMN chunk_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text';
ALTER TABLE document_chunks ADD COLUMN metadata_json TEXT;

CREATE INDEX idx_document_chunks_section
ON document_chunks(section_id, ordinal);

CREATE TABLE document_assets (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    section_id TEXT,
    blob_sha256 TEXT,
    asset_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    mime_type TEXT,
    file_name TEXT,
    metadata_json TEXT,
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES document_sections(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (blob_sha256) REFERENCES blobs(sha256) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_document_assets_document
ON document_assets(document_id, ordinal);

CREATE TABLE archive_entries (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    parent_id TEXT,
    entry_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    entry_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    entry_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        entry_type IN ('file', 'directory', 'archive', 'unsupported')
    ),
    nested_depth INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (nested_depth BETWEEN 0 AND 2),
    compression_method TEXT,
    compressed_size INTEGER,
    uncompressed_size INTEGER,
    blob_sha256 TEXT,
    nested_document_id TEXT,
    extraction_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    error_message TEXT,
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES archive_entries(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (blob_sha256) REFERENCES blobs(sha256) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (nested_document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
);

CREATE INDEX idx_archive_entries_document
ON archive_entries(document_id, ordinal);

CREATE INDEX idx_archive_entries_parent
ON archive_entries(parent_id, ordinal);

UPDATE storage_metadata SET value = '2' WHERE key = 'schema_version';
"#;

pub const MIGRATION_3_TO_4: &str = r#"

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_summaries (
    document_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    summary TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    summarizer_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'processing', 'completed', 'failed')),
    source_chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    error_message TEXT,
    created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_summaries_status
ON document_summaries(status, updated_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_summary_parts (
    document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    stage INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (stage BETWEEN 0 AND 32),
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ordinal >= 0),
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    summarizer_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (document_id, stage, ordinal),
    FOREIGN KEY (document_id) REFERENCES extracted_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_summary_parts_stage
ON document_summary_parts(document_id, stage, ordinal);

"#;
